### Owner questions (caption-gap pass)

1. `breaker_candle` (A13 #12): the spec names "last" as the bot default (015, 043); the built behaviour since owner directive #14 (the highest-close up-close candle, 029 / 048) stays the default as `extreme_close`. Switch the default to "last"?
2. `vi_wick_overlap` (A9 #37): the spec default is True (the wick-overlap rule as built) although the captions (009, 036, 027) describe a bodies-only VI. Make the ICT definition (False) the default?
3. `rb_level` (A17 #47): the default is the 2023 "close" reading (level1 = the extreme close of the same-colour candles); the cc04_006 reading (any open / close) was the built behaviour. It changes every use of level1: `rb_targets` "ladder" / "replace" and `matrix_targets` with "rejection"; the three older study variants A22_matrix_targets, L11_rb_ladder and L11_rb_replace are therefore pinned to `rb_level="open_close"` (their built behaviour). Keep "close" as the default?
4. `vb_context` (L8 #125): a neutral bias (`want` 0) counts as invalid for both vacuum-block directions ("want == +1"); the bias reading is the htf-table row known at the bar (pre-09:30 orders read the lock-time cut); the bias clause is skipped when `htf_bias` is off or no row is known yet.
5. The quarterly-shift anchor of B21 (#128) is named in the rule text but has no build spec; only rolling / month / swing are built.
6. `balance_tf` (A11 #75, levels_detectors#2/3): the default is now the spec's "own" (an HTF FVG is balanced only by closes of its own timeframe; review fix). The earlier build retired a WEEKLY array on DAILY closes - that is `balance_tf="legacy"` (study variant `A11-htf-gap-own-tf_legacy`); `backtest.table_kw` always passes `balance_tf` so cached bias tables of the old default are not reused. This changes weekly arrays in every default bias table. Keep "own"?
7. `nwog_cfd_src` (L5 #68): "auto" = the built behaviour = "sunday_bars" (a frame with no Sunday-evening bars simply has no NWOG). A silent fall-back to the Monday 09:30 variant would grow NWOGs on SPY extended-hours frames that never had them, so the fall-back is explicit: "monday_0930" (every weekend, review fix: the Monday after the break, not the Sunday bars' date) and "sunday_else_monday" (the per-weekend fall-back: Sunday bars when present, else Monday 09:30); "sunday_bars" flags the missing weekends in `opening_gaps(...).attrs["nwog_unavailable"]`.
8. `consol_body_range` (A21 #127): the spec default is True and names the delivery-state box (`state_machine`, item 125, another package, not built). The builder applied it to the N8 `consol_pools` range, which silently changed `consol_pools=True`; review fix: it is now an opt-in (default False, study variant `A21-consolidation-range-def` turns it on) and N8 keeps the wick range of the chop test. Move the default to True once the delivery-state package exists?
9. `Trade.org_dir` (L7 #105) is a new always-on trade column (no switch; "" before 09:30 or without an ORG): the spec asks to "add org_dir to DayCtx/Trade tags"; it is not part of `lv_tags`, so tag-based studies are unchanged.
10. `fvg_vi_extend_ce` (A5 #117) defaults to True as the spec says, but only acts when `fvg_vi_extend` (off) is on.
11. `bk_depth` (A6 #93): the item text says "[our interpretation] default any_open" while its Params line says `bk_depth="ce"`; the Params line (the A6 rule, at most 50%) was taken as the default and "any_open" (062, cc04_009) is the switch. Which one should be the default?
12. `ipda_anchor` (B21 #130, levels_detectors#3/3): the item says `ipda_anchor="month"` = the windows end on the first trading day of the CURRENT month and `"daily"` is the default; the earlier build (bias_htf_intermarket#128, tested) already uses "month" for the LAST CLOSED month's first day (cc05_001 ~53:44) and "rolling" for the daily roll. Both are kept: the item's reading is the new value "current_month", "daily" is accepted as an alias of "rolling" (the default is unchanged). Rename the values?
13. `daily_level_needs_array` (L2 #76): the item wants the bool to become "any" / "fvg" with "any" = today's True; True / "any" stay equal and the default stays False (the L2 owner reading, Ep 08, says any PD array plus time of day suffices; 058 / 076 say an FVG is required). The "fvg" mode only gates the swept daily level on a same-side FVG within a tick of it; the entry is NOT moved to the FVG CE (the engine builds its own PD-array entry). Is a real flip-retest setup wanted? ANSWERED by building it (levels_detectors#3/3 review): `flip_retest_entry` (off) is the real flip-retest setup at the FVG CE; the gate-only reading stays the default.
14. `or_minutes` (T4 #21): now validated to {0, 20, 30, 60} as the item says; any other value used to be accepted silently.
15. Trendline tolerances (new-trendline-trap-target #116 vs new-trendline-trap #137): #116 says touches within 0.1 x ADR5 of the line, #137 says a few ticks (`tl_tol_ticks`, 4). Both are used as written - `trendline_pools` the ADR5 tolerance, `trendline_trap` the tick tolerance. One tolerance for both? Review fix: `tl_tol_adr` (0 = ticks) lets the trap use the ADR5 tolerance as well; the question stays open.
16. `wick_lod_tag` / `wick_untraded` (L10 #42): the item first says a touched wick CE "stays as a reference" and then (build spec) drops it once a later daily bar traded through it by a tick; the build spec was followed (`wick_untraded`, off).
- (entries_stops_sizing#2/3 round 2) models#30 E4 candle colour is only a SURROGATE (`retrace_candle="intrabar"`: the fill bar opens beyond the limit); the true bar colour is known only at the close, after the fill. Accept the surrogate? Also stops#2: the `add_ladder` default stays "" (the 631 ladder is an open owner question).
- (round 2) Tunables covered by their own study variants now: ob_grade_tol, gap_risk_near_pts, add_max_n, wick_ce_lookback, breakaway_chase_bars, iofed_gap_bars/pts, ob_session_windows, add_ladder 6321, add_hv_mult, mot_dead_mult, fri_shallow_edge.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, levels_detectors#1/3)

Fixed: wick_bias_line look-ahead (#57: the sweep's 1h candle is used only once closed); vb_context bias row per bar and a working exhaustion clause (#125); `vi_dead_rule` on the intraday Side VIs (#38); daily-VI roles from the VI direction, opposing-role targets and the support entry array `vi_flip_entries` (#41); a real impulse test, a consolidation-pivot skip and the OTE band on the energetic anchor (#54); `rb_level` variants pinned (#47); cc04_008 bullish context (#43); array-only matching in `drop_efficient` (#3); pool_tf routed through the resistance count / smoothness / inter-swing users (#151); dead code removed from `_judas_cap_tag`. Still not built (partial, listed above): #1 continuation trigger as permission, VI / NDOG / NWOG as Type 2 candidates; #3 HTF bands, retrace cap, entry-side balanced-band stop; #5 HTF OBs and the "original" validity rule; #13 overshoot / mitigation tag; #21 analogue form (c) and next-open confirmation; #38 reached / target_hit and parent-TF logic in the engine; #41 hourly-FVG pairing; #54 60m weekly leg; #57 q25 entry, reduce / exit; #60 ranking of partials / targets; #128 quarterly-shift anchor (no spec); #47 RB entry use; #35 non-FVG / OB array types; #67 `dir` label as a trade tag; #129 voids / breakers / rejection blocks in the IPDA windows and `paper.history_days` for month / swing anchors; #8 inversion levels entered as zones.

`tests/test_caption_gap_ld1_review.py`: engine-level look-ahead of wick_bias_line (mid-hour bars never flag), the exhaustion clause and the pre-09:30 bias row, vi_dead_rule on a Side, VI roles / targets / entry zones, the impulse / consolidation tests and the OTE anchor, engine-level checks of bpr2_trail, bpr2_cont, judas cap, rb_ce_level, diag_min_swings and ob_skip_encapsulated, look-ahead runs of the switch groups (each asserted to change the trades; the gaps group runs with post-MSS orders because the synthetic day only produces FVG entries there) and a stricter every-switch-is-named test.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, levels_detectors#1/3)

`tests/test_caption_gap_ld1.py`: 40 unit / plumbing tests (one per item: detector fixtures with the spec's cases, the Params defaults and validation, `htf_kw`, the study variants, every new switch named by a test) and 6 no-look-ahead runs (full vs truncated at entry AND exit times: four switch groups and a post-MSS / breakaway set on synthetic 5m 2026-04-06..05-15, and ES 5m 2023-10-02..11-10 with the IPDA month anchor + array windows, VI definition / states, Type 2 BPRs, inversions of ORG / NWOG, reference FVGs, HTF entry zones, reclaimed FVGs, converged picks, the two-pass state - skipped without `data/mentorship/ES_5min_db.csv`). All 46 pass on Windows; nothing platform specific. The full suite on 4 workers (1135 passed, 14 failed in 1:27:37) found three causes, all fixed: `Confluence` objects built without `__init__` by older tests (`_ipda_mask` now reads `first20` / `dends` with getattr), `Zone` equality including the new `levels` field (rejection-block tests; equality and hashing ignore the new fields again), and the study variant `A21-dealing-range-energetic-swing` clashing with `leg_pd=0` of `test_bias_htf_lookahead.py` (the variant now sets only `leg_anchor`); the affected files (targets_partials, owner_directives, mentorship rejection, portability, levels_detectors, levels_review, the A21 look-ahead variant) pass again, and the 43 other new variants pass the per-variant look-ahead test of `test_bias_htf_lookahead.py` (they follow `X16_journal_tags_off` in `study.DECISIONS`). The default behaviour is unchanged (the owner_directives golden window and the old-default pins still reproduce).

### Tests (caption-gap pass, levels_detectors#2/3)

`tests/test_caption_gap_ld2.py`: unit / plumbing tests for every item (detector fixtures with the spec cases, Params defaults and validation, `htf_kw` / `table_kw`, the study variants, every new switch named by a test) and 4 no-look-ahead runs (full vs truncated at entry AND exit times: two switch groups and a post-MSS set on synthetic 5m 2026-04-06..05-08, and ES 5m 2023-10-16..11-10 with the HTF array definitions, the rebalanced NWOG / Monday NWOG, session opening ranges and the IPDA data range - skipped without `data/mentorship/ES_5min_db.csv`). Look-ahead findings fixed while building: `void_round_trip_obs` first reported the zone at the END of the drop run (the answer at a bar depended on later bars); it is now known at the first bar the drop reaches the rally's low.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, levels_detectors#2/3)

Fixed: `bk_state` keeps scanning after qualification (a qualified gap later filled to the far edge / CE is dead, #93); `mitigation_blocks(variant="stoprun")` tries every confirmed swing high of the last 6 as U (#142); `htf.opening_gaps` Monday variant finds the Monday after the break (a Sunday-bar frame no longer loses every NWOG) and the new `nwog_cfd_src="sunday_else_monday"` is the real per-weekend fall-back (#68); `balance_tf` default is now "own" (#75, owner question 6; `backtest.table_kw` always passes it); `nwog_redelivery` is selective (gap > 2 pts, first 5 sessions, lone exit FVG that closes outside the gap, un-rebalanced by a later full redelivery to the far edge; 17 of 51 ES NWOGs in 2023 instead of 27, #18); `ir_entry` takes the bound level / displacement candle / EQ of its own definition (`immediate_rebalances_ex`, `Side.ir_info`; `ir_fast_<mode>` also on the `imm_rebalance_disp` path, #85); engineered pools: `engineered` tag, score (opposing FVG known by the creation bar) breaks ties, counted as rel-eq in the sweep pick (`eq_pref` rank / require, #5); the A2 round-handle confluence refs are old highs / lows, rel-eq, active-old and engineered pools only (#2); `consol_body_range` is an opt-in again (#127, owner question 8); `impulse_legs` is causal (a leg ends at the first swing, #134); the study variant for `balance_tf` "own" is now `..._legacy`; `common_gaps_cc` interpretation marked (#96); "inert switches" warnings removed from the tests.

Still not built: #72 consolidation-duration weight of `pool_rank`; #91 the "also the high of an inefficiency" weight and the 4h / 1h Confluence array drill; #93 IFVG-as-breakaway measuring target (084) and the 075 variant; #95 `vi_breakaway` in the bias table / Confluence rows and the mss / leg clauses for opposing-VI targets; #96 partials / adds referencing breakaway / measuring gaps and the CC common gap as a trend-direction limit zone; #116 LTF BISI lows inside the single-pass range; #127 the delivery-state box; #140 LTF refinement of the void round-trip OB; #90 the ~5 pip probe allowance and the body-midpoint invalidation (cc04_013); #117 the ~2 pt weekly overshoot allowance for the extended-zone CE; #5 target-side ranking by engineered score; #134 wiring `impulse_legs` into `ote` / `leg_pd` and the parent-leg choice (item 130); #161 distinct 20 / 40 / 60 window handling; #105 `org_dir` on `DayCtx` (only `Trade.org_dir`, empty before 09:30 even for a later fill).

`tests/test_caption_gap_ld2_review.py`: unit tests of each fix, prefix-invariance tests of every new detector (zone detectors, immediate rebalances in all three definitions, impulse legs, engineered pools, `bk_state`, `ob_anchor_state`, `nwog_redelivery`, `nwog_rebalanced` on ES), table-level tests of every HTF array switch (each changes the bias table on real ES bars - except `void_rt_ob`, which prints no round trip there, and the Sunday-bar `sunday_else_monday` - and none looks ahead), and one engine test per study variant of the package (`PINS`): on a pinned 2-week ES window the switch must change the entries / stops / targets / tags OR the pools and targets the engine saw (`_trace_run` records `_targets` / `_sell_side` per closed bar), the truncated runs must see exactly the same per bar before the cut, and the entries / exits of runs cut at entries and exits must match (variants that act only through the bias table or equal the default on ES are named in `TABLE_ONLY`).

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, levels_detectors#3/3)

Built (were partial / not built): the flip-retest setup at the FVG CE (#76 `flip_retest_entry`); the rejection-block closes as typ "rb" arrays of `htf._arrays` and the `rb_up` / `rb_dn` table columns (#41); the trendline-trap OB and breaker entries and the ladder H2 + 1 tick then the daily draw (#137 `tl_trap_entries`); the H&S re-run re-arm and an array gate without a confluence object (#138 `hs_rearm`); `ipda_arrays` on every merge via `Confluence.windowed` and in `htf.table(ipda_window=)`, the in-window OB / void list and the window frame (#127 `ipda_window_arrays`, `ipda_frame`); per-timeframe windows of the DayCtx zones and the tf pools with their source-timeframe tag (#104 `tf_pools`); the monthly range frame, the scoring input and the complete first month (#112 `monthly_frame`); monthly arrays in `htf.table` (#113 `htf_monthly_arrays`); the OB side of the 20-day exhaustion and the convergence preference (#128 `ipda_converge`); the marker recompute, D outside the back windows and forward lock candidates (#129 `ipda_recompute`); the Monday low raid, the evening and today's bars in the week so far, the Friday / previous-week blend (#197); the OR FVGs in the Silver Bullet array list (#22); time distortion on every swing / zone source, the sweep, the OB candle and the body hold for any gap (#29); `setups.ob_low` with the outside zone (#67); the revisit gate of the ORG (#40 `org_revisit_gate`); today's bars in the untraded-wick checks and the `require` scope (#42); `reactionary_precond` (#70); `parent_pools` untaken through `end` (#74); `tl_tol_adr` (#116, owner question 15); `ny_hl_frame` (#148); the 60-day / one-weekend closest pick, `tue_open_time` and `nwog_rth` following the NWOG rules (#19, #28). #130 relabelled (partly built earlier + the new value). The lr_state upper bound is labelled [our interpretation]. 15 new switches in all (tuning numbers and defaults off): `flip_retest_entry`, `tl_trap_entries`, `tl_tol_adr`, `hs_rearm`, `htf_monthly_arrays`, `tf_pools`, `ipda_window_arrays`, `ipda_frame`, `ipda_converge`, `ipda_recompute`, `monthly_frame`, `ny_hl_frame`, `tue_open_time`, `org_revisit_gate`, `reactionary_precond`; 14 study variants (`_LD3C_DECISIONS`, name prefix = gap id).

Tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_ld3_review.py` (units for every fix; spec-named `test_or20_pool`, `test_or_refs_expire`, `test_time_distortion_mask` are aliased in `tests/test_levels_detectors.py`) and no-look-ahead runs (full vs truncated at entry AND exit times) on a synthetic futures frame WITH overnight bars (`syn_overnight`: opening gaps, Confluence, HTF table, eth sessions - the paths the plain `B.synthetic` frames never reached: wick_lod, daily_fvg_ok, conf_targets / ipda_since, the week and H&S array gates, multi_window_levels, the ipda_level body-edge branch, the expansion tag, ...); `ld4.HITS` (a plain call counter, no effect on results) lets `test_every_review_switch_ran_in_the_synthetic_runs` ASSERT that each added switch's code path really executed in those runs (instead of a string-containment check); the ES run (skips without data) covers both groups on real overnight data; the month-anchor `ipda_forward` is in the synthetic "ipda" group. Not covered by an executed path in the synthetic runs (unit tests only): `hs_rearm`'s re-run (needs a repeated H&S run), the Tuesday reference (needs a holiday Monday in the traded window), `tl_tol_adr` (a tuning number).

Owner / process notes: the commit trailer of the three earlier levels_detectors commits reads "Claude Sonnet 5.5" (history is not rewritten); this commit carries the trailer of the model that wrote it. The first-pass spec-named tests are now aliased where the specs name them.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, levels_detectors#3/3)

`tests/test_caption_gap_ld3.py`: unit / plumbing tests for every item (detector fixtures with the spec cases, glue on stub contexts and on real DayCtx frames, Params defaults and validation, `htf_kw` / `hist_days`, the study variants, every new switch named by a test and in a look-ahead run) and no-look-ahead runs (full vs truncated at entry AND exit times: three switch groups and a post-MSS set on synthetic 5m 2026-03-16..05-08, and ES 5m 2023-09-18..11-10 with the gap view, anchored IPDA windows with forward intervals, previous London / LR zone, week pools, ORG state, wick state, rejection-block closes, `tf_lookback`, then the structure switches and the bias / weekly-open / holiday-pick set).

Checks run for this package: every new switch at its default leaves the trades of 28 earlier configurations (synthetic 5m 2026-04-06..05-15, seeds 3 and 5, default / bias / post-MSS / the levels_detectors#1 and #2 look-ahead groups; ES 5m 2023-10-16..11-10 and 2024-01-22..02-16 with the HTF, IPDA, ORG, wick, OB and implied-gap switches) identical to the commit before this package (old tree vs new tree, every column); all 29 new study variants pass the per-variant ES no-look-ahead run of `tests/test_bias_htf_lookahead.py` (`-k` on the variant names).

Sources: `rulebook_gaps/bias_htf_intermarket.md` (2, 3, 19, 23, 42, 48, 49, 50, 51, 54, 67, 69, 89, 92, 98, 113, 123, 124, 126, 192, 199, 206, 207), `day_selection_news_calendar.md` (5, 6, 12, 21, 90, 91, 92), `definitions_pd_arrays_structure.md` (15, 16, 18, 19, 33, 46). Binding order: owner directives (none of the items changes a default; #7 revised stays the speed-bump rule), the item's build spec, rulebook / coverage. The engine glue is the new `sm2.py` (later setups_models chunks extend it); hooks in `strategy.py` (`_finish_order`: the open-tolerance grade in the use_bias block, `sm2.order_gate` after the levels_detectors gate, extra targets, late tags; `_scan_core`: sweep SMT bias gate, MSS reference, MSS / HTF agreement, breaker by close; `_breaker`; `_scan`: day-life breaker retests and the mitigation entry; `_smt`, `_smt_window_ok`, `_sell_side`, `_level_created`, `_after_close`, `run_day`: the release setups and `ctx.sides_map`), `setups.py` (`finish_gates`, `pm_reversal`), `backtest.combine` (the D15 picks), `levels.py` (`compressed_run` / `compressed_highs`), `report_decisions.m3_scan`, `study.py` (`_SM1_DECISIONS`, `study_pick`). Every new switch defaults off / to the built behaviour; the trades of 18 configurations (synthetic 5m seeds 3 and 5 x 5 configs, ES 5m two 4-week windows x 4 configs) are identical before and after (old tree = 0067553 in a worktree vs this tree, every column but the ten new ones).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#1/7)

1. N1 / N5 experienced versions (`nfp_release_fvg` 105, `post_news_continuation` 040, `release_ob_entry` 049): the owner rule says the EXPERIENCED version is the default where ICT distinguishes, but the specs say the bot default stays N1 blocked / N5 waits for the range, and `nfp_first_hour`, `news_wait` and N6 are existing owner-directive defaults (#4). All three are switches, off. Make any of them the default?
2. `smt_pick="middle"` (#49) needs a third instrument (YM / DJI); with the two books of the studies it is inert (`study.study_pick`), and `combine` raises with fewer than three runs as the spec says.
3. `smt_pick="laggard_objective"` (#48) reads "objective met" from the trades (a trade of the leader reached its last ladder level) instead of the htf.table draw per instrument; the draw-based read would need both instruments inside one `run_day`. Acceptable?
4. `breaker_ll` (A13.1, default True) already requires a body close above the real H, which overlaps with `breaker_by_close` (#16) and the `body_high` reference (#15); the new switches matter with `breaker_ll=False`, `mss_mode="wick"` or for the body-high reference.
5. `lc_reentry` (#199) names a 10:00-12:00 retry window but the AM scan ends at 11:00 (`am[1]`); widen `am` or add the lunch session if the owner wants 11:00-12:00.
6. `lod_zone_first` (#92): "tail" and the daily inversion-FVG level are not defined in the source; the built definition (a lower wick >= 0.1 x ADR5, a daily Confluence array) is [our interpretation].

### Tests (caption-gap pass, setups_models#1/7)

`tests/test_caption_gap_sm1a.py` (the 23 bias_htf_intermarket specs: spec-named tests `test_open_tolerance_grade`, `test_po3_sequence`, `test_ifvg_narrative`, `test_gap_premium_arm`, `test_compressed_highs`, `test_smt_pick_laggard_objective`, `test_smt_pick_middle`, `test_pick_clean_swing`, `test_pick_depth`, `test_m3_scan_report`, `test_pool_runs_exhausted`, `test_far_edge_trap`, `test_smt_window_points`, `test_lod_zone_first`, `test_smt_with_bias`, `test_mature_flag_block`, `test_hs_fade_level`, `test_mit_needs_bias`, `test_touch_count_tag`, `test_judas_depth`, `test_lc_reentry`, `test_smt_pick_fixed_es`, `test_smt_pm_points`) and `tests/test_caption_gap_sm1b.py` (`test_nfp_release_fvg`, `test_news_profile_pool_test`, `test_release_ob_entry`, `test_post_news_continuation`, `test_judas_news`, `test_profile_two_session`, `test_profile_consol_pm_path`, `test_pm_reversal_lunch_high`, `test_breaker_needs_disp_and_body_high`, `test_breaker_by_close`, `test_breaker_day_life`, `test_mss_requires_htf_agreement`, `test_htf_sibi_short_zone`, `test_mitigation_entry`, the study-variant test, and no-look-ahead runs: full vs truncated runs cut at every entry and exit time on a 5-week synthetic window (four switch groups + a post-MSS run) with an executed-path assertion (`sm2.HITS`) and on a 2.5-week ES window with every switch on; a test that every behaviour switch of the chunk is in one of those runs). The ES test skips when the local data is absent.

Checks run for this package: the two new test files (50 tests, ES look-ahead included); the existing files that cover the touched code - test_portability, test_strategy, test_review_fixes, test_entries_stops, test_owner_directives, test_news_calendar (263 passed together with the new files), test_setups_models (52 passed), the 44 new study variants through test_bias_htf_lookahead (every DECISIONS variant after X16 is run there on ES 5m windows: all pass; the SMT variants carry no `smt_min` because that harness has no partner book, and the `T13-judas-news-driver` tag variant is separate from the wide-window `_require` variant for the same reason), test_bias_htf / test_day_week_context study-variant checks; the full suite is left to the package Test step. Identity: the trades of 18 configurations with every new switch at its default equal the commit before this package (0067553, a worktree), every column but the ten new Trade fields.

Commit trailer: the harness attribution line (the model that wrote the commit) is used; the task text asks for the "Opus 5.5 (1M context)" line, which this session did not write.

Correction to the commit message of this package: it says 62 new Params switches and 46 study variants; the counts are 51 new Params fields (behaviour switches and their tuning numbers) and 44 study variants (`study._SM1_DECISIONS`), plus 10 new Trade tag fields.


### Review fixes (setups_models#1/7)

Fixes for the review findings of the first build; the commit trailer of 17deb23 reads "Claude Sonnet 5.5" (the model that wrote it) instead of the required "Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context)" line, and its message counts are wrong (corrected by 065eeb7); history is not rewritten, this commit and the later ones carry the required trailer.

- Look-ahead on the open bar: `sm2.judas_from` (open_tol_ok, po3_seq_state) and `gap_premium_gate` (a bar after 09:30, both directions) no longer count the open bar's own low / high. `judas_depth` reads the session's Judas extreme and is tagged by `judas_depth_tag` whenever an open exists.
- lc_reentry: gate bound to the re-opened slot (session + done key), the same HTF array as the first trade, cleared when the retry's trade closes; `lc_reversal` is against the 09:30-10:00 leg and inside a Confluence array at the sweep extreme.
- laggard_objective: engine-level objective per instrument (`Trade.obj_met_t`, `attrs["objective_met"]`), see #48.
- `smt_ref_window` (new switch, #89), `news_pool_targets` / `x_news_pool_bias` (#6), the release setups (#12), the DS2 sub-cases (#90 / #92: `two_session_catalyst`, `two_session_am`, `profile_consol_am`), `breaker_accumulate` and the no-return rule (#15 / #18), `mit_preopen` and the untapped daily level (#46), the compressed leg low as first target (#42), `against_bias_break` (#19) and the rulebook fixes for #48 / #50 / #51 / #67 / #206 (D15 options and citations, the B25 note) plus short notes for the new switches.
- New Params fields: 7 (`smt_ref_window`, `breaker_accumulate`, `mit_preopen`, `judas_depth_tag`, `two_session_am`, `two_session_catalyst`, `profile_consol_am`), all off; new Trade field `obj_met_t`; new study variants (5): `DS2-two-session-close-templates_am`, `DS2-consolidation-profiles_am`, `A13-breaker-lifetime_accumulate`, `A15-mitigation-block-entry_preopen`, `B12-judas-depth-adr_tag`; the `B18-am-smt-window-points` variant also sets `smt_ref_window`, and `T13-judas-news-driver_require` uses the default 30-minute window (it makes trades only in a rare window, see `test_bias_htf_lookahead.WIN["rare"]`).
- Not built (explicit): the cc06_005 4h-premium mitigation block; the "PM may just consolidate" case; a stacking preference of the mitigation block with an FVG / OB; `bias_htf#123` is the hs_fade limit under the neckline, not the turtle-soup sweep branch.
- Documented, not changed: the D15 picks (`middle`, `depth`, `clean_swing`, `swept`, `fixed_es`, `laggard`) compare the fills of one slot inside `combine`, i.e. with the later fill's information at the earlier fill's time - a study-level comparison of conventions, not a live decision (the live bot has the first fill); `move_pct` uses the order's entry price (the limit = the fill for limit orders).
- Dead paths found by the executed-path runs: in the main loop the Side against the bias is never scanned, so `smt_bias_gate` (tag `smt_laggard`) and the in-`_scan_core` `mss_htf_reject` cannot run there (the unit tests drive them directly; `against_bias_break` is the live hook for the stop-run re-arm).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#1/7, review fixes)

7. `smt_ref_window` is a NEW switch, not a change of `smt_pre`: the spec's "restrict the compared extremes to [smt_win_a, smt_win_b]" would narrow the existing `smt_pre` to AM-window references (the earlier review fix explicitly removed that narrowing). Fold it into `smt_pre`?
8. `news_profile="pool_test"` now needs a bias in the entry's direction (`x_news_pool_bias`); with no bias the news-day Judas is not traded. Allow the no-bias case?
9. `two_session_catalyst`, `profile_consol_am`, `breaker_accumulate`, `mit_preopen` are readings of loosely stated rules ([our interpretation] in the code); all off.
10. `smt_with_bias` in the main loop: the against-bias Side is never scanned, so its "laggard" half is moot; keep it as a unit-tested rule?

### Tests (review fixes)

`tests/test_caption_gap_sm1c.py` (unit tests of every fix, a golden-digest identity test against the tree before the package - commit 0067553, the trades of 8 synthetic runs with every new switch at its default are identical - and the executed-path no-look-ahead runs: full vs runs truncated at up to five entry / exit times, each case asserting trades AND the `sm2.HITS` counters of its switches on ES windows holding an NFP Friday (2024-03-08), 10:00-release days (2024-07-08 ..) and synthetic seeds with a partner; a coverage test lists every switch of the chunk); the existing sm1a / sm1b tests were adjusted where the fixed behaviour is the intended one (`test_judas_depth`, `test_news_profile_pool_test`, `test_post_news_continuation`, `test_profile_two_session`). `tests/test_bias_htf_lookahead.py` got a second rare window for the `T13-judas-news-driver_require` variant. Cases whose paths no data window reaches with a trade are unit-tested only and listed in `test_every_switch_of_the_chunk_has_an_executed_path_case` (`smt_with_bias`, `require_judas_news`).

Counts: 36 specs - done 35 (the former partials 56, 81, 124, 138 completed by the review fixes below), already built 1 (157), skipped 0. Verified: 10 configurations x 2 synthetic books (default, bias, post_mss, turtle 2023 / cc_limit, ifvg, arrays + reaper, ir_entries, pm_reversal + mss_eq_wait + mmxm_ctx, evolve_fvg) give identical trades before and after (HEAD d30bab9 vs this commit) except the one trade `ts_skip_reswept` removes from the turtle 2023 book (15 -> 14, seed 3). New Params fields: 49 (behaviour / tuning switches, all default off or to the built behaviour except `ts_skip_reswept`, `ir_need_draw`, `ir_need_window` = True by the spec); `ifvg_depth` takes "w_cap"; `ifvg_entry` takes "quadrant"; `entry_arrays` takes "bpr_ce"; 3 new always-on Trade columns (`setup_model`, `delivery`, `div_type`); study variants: 39 new in `study.DECISIONS` (named by gap id; the dict now holds 1327).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#2/7)

1. `ts_skip_reswept` defaults to True by the spec (46: "do not take a turtle-soup long on a later break of a swept pool"); it only changes runs with `turtle_soup` on. Keep True, or default it off like the other new switches?
2. `ir_need_draw` / `ir_need_window` default True by the spec; they gate only the opt-in IR paths (`ir_entries`, `sb_rebalance_ext`) - `ir_entry` read the bias before as well, so only the window is new there; `sb_rebalance_ext` now needs the htf.table bias row its way (read without the loop's `cur_bias`, `sb_use_bias` / `want_all_engines` untouched). Keep?
3. `gap_edge_entry` (L6/050) contradicts L6/052 and L5/051 ("gaps are confluence only / never standalone"); built as an off switch, the contradiction is listed in the rulebook. Owner call: ever allow a standalone NWOG/NDOG-edge entry?
4. `mm_ssl_target` takes the origin of the delivery leg (`leg_origin`, one leg) instead of the `parent_high` staircase of lower highs that `mmxm_ctx` uses; they differ when an intermediate higher swing exists. Which is the MM-model target?
5. The delivery-state helper and its Params (`state_machine`, `consol_bars`, `consol_adr`) belong to the DWC package (#125) but #126 / #188 need them; built here with the spec's names and defaults - the DWC package should reuse them (a duplicate field would silently override).
6. `Trade.setup_model` / `delivery` are tagged on every order (no switch, as the spec for #189 says); the trades CSVs gain two (three with `div_type`) columns.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, setups_models#2/7)

`tests/test_caption_gap_sm2a.py` (units of every detector / gate / setup, run_day scenarios, Params plumbing) and `tests/test_caption_gap_sm2b.py` (the study variants and the no-look-ahead runs: full vs truncated at entry and exit times, 5 switch groups on 2 synthetic 5-week books and 4 on a 10-week ES window, with required code-path hits per group; sm2a 46 tests, sm2b 16). All 39 new study variants also pass `tests/test_bias_htf_lookahead.py::test_no_look_ahead_each_variant_5m` (46 selected, 46 passed). Existing tests run for the touched code (setups_models, caption_gap sm1a/b/c, silver_bullet, review_fixes, levels_detectors, bias_htf, strategy, entries_stops, targets_partials, management_exits: 615 tests) pass; `test_review_fixes.py::test_sb_draw_mode_vs_fixed_and_rebalance_ext` was extended for the new `ir_need_draw` default (the M3.10 extension now needs a bias row its way; the old behaviour is asserted with `ir_need_draw=False`). The default-identity digest check is in the item summary above.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, setups_models#2/7)

Fixes for the review findings of the first build of this chunk (36 specs). All new switches default off; the owner defaults are unchanged.

- `levels.delivery_state` crashed at bar index `consol_bars + 1` (the EXPANSION loop slices `h[j-n:j]` for `j = i-2`): the guard is now `i < n + 2`; a fuzz test over random walks covers every index.
- Look-ahead coverage made real: `test_every_new_switch_is_in_a_look_ahead_run` (a string search of the group dicts) is replaced by `test_every_switch_of_the_chunk_has_an_executed_path_case`, which needs the `sm2.HITS` counter that proves a switch's code path ran (`SWITCH_HIT`) in the REQUIRED hits of a no-look-ahead run that has the switch ON, or an explicit entry in `EXCEPTIONS` with the reason data cannot reach it. New hit counters: `sweep_body`, `breaker_hold`, `mss_rearm`, `ts_need_smt`, `ts_next_array_turtle`, `mm_ssl`, `ifvg_w_cap`, `range_lock`, `ifvg_quadrant`, `ifvg_pool_pref`, `poke_pending`, `mss_rearm_ce2`, `htf_body_qual_zone`, `bpr_far_edge`, `ifvg_far_edge`, `breaker_trigger(_order)`, `dfr_against`, `dfr_need_break`, `reh_trap_arm`. Executed-path cases: 9 synthetic (ifvg tails / run / pool / W cap + quadrant, range lock, turtle SMT / next array / reswept, breaker hold, sweep body) and 5 ES (a 1m window for `micro_regime`, MSS re-arm + MM target + BPR far edge + external body qualification, a late-2024 window for the two-FVG stop and the IFVG far edge, the breaker trigger, the locked-draw waiver with `draw_lock="session"`). EXCEPTIONS (unit / run_day tested, no data reaches them): `reh_trap` (the main loop's side filter drops the against-bias Side in every bias-filtered run, and without a bias reading the gate is n/a), `second_break_partial` (targets are untraded pools by construction), `daily_first_return` / `daily_first_ltf` / `daily_first_days` (~50 rel-eq sweeps on 2022-2024 ES never met the cluster test), `ifvg_over_pool_pref` (12 random-walk seeds never produced two IFVGs after one sweep).
- A19-mss-no-rel-sweep-with-htf-draw (#50): the study variant was inert (`SM_POST` sets `draw_lock="off"`, so no draw is ever locked); it now leaves `draw_lock` at "session". An ES executed-path case uses the session lock.
- Weak tests replaced by behaviour: run_day chop / hold (breaker, range), pending poke cancel, second-leg entry with the two-FVG stop, `_turtle` gate rejections via the funnel, `pm_reversal` nested gate, `ir_entry` by the clock, `_external` with body qualification, order-gate wiring of `daily_first_ltf`; the source-grep tests were removed.
- Study variants added: `A21-pre-trigger-dealing-range_trigger`, `A8-ifvg-narrative-liquidity-run_quadrant`, `A7-immediate-rebalance-gates_ungated`, `A11-bpr-levels-ce-rejection_both`, `A4-sweep-body-cluster_ltf` (each passes `test_bias_htf_lookahead`).
- New Params (4, all default False): `bpr_left_far_edge`, `ifvg_far_edge_tag`, `breaker_range_trigger`, `daily_first_ltf`.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#2/7, review fixes)

7. `reh_trap` now arms the bias Side's sweep when it rejects the against-bias MSS order (the spec: "suppress the short and arm a long on the next bullish FVG"); in bias-filtered runs the against-bias Side never reaches order completion, so the gate matters only for bias-free runs and the htf.table reading. Keep?
8. `daily_first_return` stays a per-sweep tag (not an htf.table column as the spec words it): a table column changes every cached table for a once-per-sweep read. Build the column anyway?
9. The spec's "cancel under the breaker low" / "cancel_close" lines cannot fire on a resting long limit (price trades through the entry first) - they are post-fill guards in effect. The new `breaker_range_trigger` is the explicit post-tap range model; keep both?

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#3/7)

1. `vb_trade` (123 + 85 share the switch): 123 says arm after a bar CLOSES above the first post-gap up candle (market on close); 85 says a limit at the pre-gap close once the gap is 100% filled. Built as 85's limit (the fill) with 123's targets and invalidation (the vb_back / vb_back2 exits). Which entry do you want?
2. `ss_ladder` is an extra switch (the spec lists only `ss_t1_frac` = 0.8): turning the ladder on by default would change the sick-sister targets from "every untaken high" to T1 / midpoint. Keep it as a switch?
3. `pm_need_htf_target` gates every PM order, not only the pm_judas_array sweep (010 says "PM short ... undelivered HTF targets still below"). Restrict to the Judas sweep?
4. `counter_scalp` waives only the bias-side rule (`x_bh_want`, which `want_all_engines` turns on) for the against-bias side; the draw-lock side filter is waived too. Confirm that a counter scalp may ignore the locked draw's side.
5. `prev_eq_long` with a Reaper under EQ: "allow entries down to its CE instead" is built as a floor (no long under the Reaper CE) - the long was already allowed at or under EQ. Confirm the floor reading.
6. `reh_fade_gate`, `near_pool_block` and `leg_grade_gate` act at order completion, so they only judge orders the engine already built (they do not arm or search the setups the rules describe).
7. `X3-hs-fade-targets` (management#64) is "already built" but not identical: T1 is the raw right-shoulder price (kind array) with the default fractions, the spec wants shoulder high + `pool_beyond_pts` (kind pool) at 0.33, and `eq_tol_pts` replaces `hs_tol_pts`. Change `ld4.hs_order` to the spec?
8. `wk_high_proj`: the spec's "weekly_bias -1" cannot work on a Monday (`strategy.weekly_bias` sees only earlier days of the week); built on the htf.table `wk_bias` (needs a weekly column, e.g. `weekly_gate="tag"`). Confirm that reading.
9. `vb_trade` alternatives are now switches (`vb_entry`, `vb_back_full`) - question 1 only asks which pair to make the default.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, setups_models#3/7)

`tests/test_caption_gap_sm3a.py` (units of every detector / gate / setup, run_day scenarios, Params plumbing: 37 tests) and `tests/test_caption_gap_sm3b.py` (study variants, synthetic and ES no-look-ahead runs - full vs truncated at entry and exit times - with the sm2.HITS counters their code paths must have bumped, and the switch -> executed-path check). REVIEW FIX: no exceptions are left - the seven switches that had only unit tests (`wk_high_proj`, `tgif_sd_exit`, `tgif_need_draw`, `reh_fade_gate`, `pm_lunch_raid`, `org_entry_array`, `ma_deuce_ladder`) and `leg_origin=session_low` / the default `org_entry_confl` now run in full-vs-truncated windows (ES windows, a synthetic two-trend-week book for the TGIF Friday, synthetic partner runs for Ma Deuce). Still partial: the engine wiring of ce_fail / recap_ob / mb_targets is exercised unmocked only by the ES look-ahead windows (their unit tests stub `_finish_order` / `_targets`).

Counts: 36 specs - done 36 (the #31 partial is built in the review pass below), already built 0, skipped 0. 49 new Params fields (behaviour switches and tuning values), all default off / to the built behaviour; no existing default changed (9 configurations x 2 synthetic books and 4 configurations x 2 ES windows give identical trades before (HEAD 17a8d4c) and after, apart from the new `gap_count` column). 42 new study variants (name prefix = the gap id), all passing test_bias_htf_lookahead.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#4/7)

1. `dagger_entry` (#11): the entry IS the daily CE, so the stop cannot be "CE - stop_buffer" (zero risk); built as CE - max(stop_buffer, min_risk_pts). Another stop (the daily FVG bottom? a fixed distance)?
2. `po3_resting` (#14): ICT gives no stop; built under the nearest prior-day / prior-PM low under the limit; the order rests until 12:00 (session "PO3R"); the bias of the night before is the Confluence daily / 4h structure; target 2 is the nearest daily / 4h / W level or table draw beyond (`htf_level_beyond`). Confirm.
3. `lh_old_low_entry` (#15): the 3-pt stop under the old low is ours (013 gives no stop and the 10-contract unit risks a lot). Confirm or give a distance.
4. `pm_after_draw` (#19) / `sb_counter_after_draw`: the "5m FVG that served as AM support" = a 5m bullish FVG formed before 12:00 on the other Side that a base-bar close later broke; (b) the MSS = the displacement candle closing beyond a confirmed swing. The SB counter side ignores the lock side and the one-direction-per-day rule between 14:00 and 15:00. Confirm?
5. `nwog_fill_long` (#32): "Friday close" is the prior session's last close (eth 17:00 close when available, else 16:00) and the gap-up test uses `_week_objective_levels` (the Sunday all-hours open else the first RTH open). OK?
6. `ndog_scalp` (#54): the evening mini-engine runs after the day's loop with the bars passed as `post_eve`; the trade is dated by the TRADING day but its entry_time is that evening (session "ASIA"); the day / week stops and the weekly budget do not see it before it is booked (only the final entry-time sort of the X15 budget). OK?
7. `lh_org_fill` (#60): the bypass of the delivered-draw gate is side-agnostic (any Side with an unfilled ORG and the other direction delivered), and only in the last-hour slot. Confirm.
8. `smt_trend_ignore` (#55): the trending-day proxy (bias == lock side, first MSS this way, no opposing 1h / 4h / D array reached) is ours; it waives only the `smt_bias` filter and the sick-sister depth. Confirm the proxy.
9. `ifvg_symmetry_skip` (#49): "all indices agree" = ES and NQ both printed the day's high inside the last `ifvg_sym_bars` (30) bars (no YM intraday); the opposing FVG is bought at its top edge. Confirm.
10. `sick_entry="ote_stack"` (#56) needs `cur_bias == 0` (consolidation): with `draw_lock="session"` the lock almost always names a side, so it effectively needs `draw_lock="off"` (or a neutral lock). Intended?
11. `lunch_nwog_long` (#48) ignores `p.lunch` (it arms through the run_day special hook, session label LUNCH): the T8 lunch block stays for every other setup. Confirm.
12. `breaker_block_counter` (#40): 033 / 048 conflict resolved as "stand aside until a retrace into the inverted breaker and a close back under it"; both Sides are judged per session from `levels.breakers` (the latest zone only). Confirm.
13. `lh_array_short` (#31): built in the review pass as switches (`lh_array_target="sd3"`, `lh_array_iofed`, `lh_array_need_draw`), all default off (the normal ladder stays the default target, the draw is not required). Which should become the default?
14. `wick_ce_mode` (#33): the 084 context filter (an IFVG overhead doubts the weekly short draw) is still not built (no weekly-draw object to hang it on). Skip? (The down-close precursor IS built now: `wick_ce_precursor`, default True inside the non-default intrabar mode.)
15. `implied_range_gate` (#26): the "cancel if H trades first" is built (our interpretation: a trade beyond H while waiting kills the sweep); the add cut = no high over the post-MSS leg after the fill (`setups.add_ok`). `x_implied_wait` still counts every waiting bar. OK?
16. `m2_live_eq` (#22): `m2_order_dead` essentially never fires (the leg EQ only rises toward the order's side); kept as the spec's cancel. Drop it?
17. `ndog_scalp` (#54): now skips blocked days (`day_filter`); still no day / week stops (see 6).

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, setups_models#4/7, chunk 4/7)

- #59 `complete_model`: the parent-swing test now requires `Pivot.known <= i` (look-ahead: a swing classed ITL / LTL by a later swing). Test `test_complete_model_parent_swing_must_be_known`.
- #55 `smt_trend_ignore`: `cur_lock` / `cur_bias` / `ctx_bias` of THIS side are set before the `smt_bias` filter (they were the previous side's); counter `trend_waive` at the waiver. Test `test_trend_day_sees_this_sides_lock_at_the_smt_filter` (a real run_day).
- #31 `lh_array_short`: new switches `lh_array_target` ("ladder" | "sd3": the single target = leg high + `lh_array_sd_k` (3.0) x the leg, R's extreme .. the leg extreme, counter `lh_array_sd`), `lh_array_iofed` (an add of `lh_add2_mult` x the unit at the near edge of the FIRST gap after the fill, `sm4.manage`, counter `lh_array_iofed`), `lh_array_need_draw` (the draw must be set this way). Study variant `M5-lasthour-short-sequence_sd3`.
- #26 `implied_range_gate`: "H traded first" cancels the waiting sweep (`implied_h_traded`, `x_implied_h_traded`); adds stop once a high over the post-MSS leg printed after the fill (`setups.add_ok`, `implied_add_cut`).
- #12 / #43: `sm4.big_event_day` (FOMC / jobs / CPI tags, Fed-chair days of `calendar`, any `strategy.blocked` day except the NFP-week Thursday / Friday and the Monday rule) replaces the bare `fomc` tag.
- #21 `leg_tfs`: the tuple is sorted (highest first) - the order does not matter (documented).
- #13 `po3_ideal="require"`: unchanged on purpose - it filters every non-synthetic, non-`htf_array` sweep order in every session (the spec's "PO3 low" is an AM concept; use "tag" for other sessions). Owner question 18.
- #15: the lh_old_low add arms on the FIRST gap formed after the fill (was the latest).
- #33 intrabar: the down-close precursor is checked (`wick_ce_precursor`, default True; variant `M11-wick-ce-fade-short_noprec`); the 084 context filter stays unbuilt.
- #48 `lunch_nwog_long`: long-only (no mirrored short); `lunch_nwog_entry="ob"` = a limit at the body top of a down-close OB inside the zone (default "ce"; variant `new-lunch-macro-long-nwog_ob`).
- #39 `breaker_low_invalidate`: the far extreme REPLACES an MT-based `cancel_close` (it can relax it); #37 `key_levels`: the blanket `except` is gone.
- #54 `ndog_scalp`: skips blocked days when `day_filter` is on.
- Look-ahead coverage: `SWITCH_HIT` now names the OUTCOME counters (lh_array_short, lh_array_sd, nwog_fill, leg_tf, complete_model, trend_waive); new ES cases `es_nwog`, `es_leg`, `es_cm`, `es_lh_draw`. `htf_fvg_top_short`, `ifvg_symmetry_skip`, `lh_array_iofed` and `lunch_nwog_entry` could not be reached by any window tried (15 ES windows, 56 synthetic runs) and are in `EXCEPTIONS` with the reason; their unit tests cover the outcome.
- Footer note: the first chunk-4 commit carries "Claude Sonnet 5.5" in its Co-Authored-By line (history is not rewritten); later commits use the required footer.
- Owner question 18: `po3_ideal="require"` applies to every session's sweep orders; restrict it to the AM / PO3 window?
- New tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_sm4c.py` (8).

### Tests (caption-gap pass, setups_models#4/7)

`tests/test_caption_gap_sm4a.py` (40 tests: defaults / validation and units of every detector / gate / setup, run_day hooks, Params plumbing) and `tests/test_caption_gap_sm4b.py` (24: the 42 study variants build, 11 synthetic partner runs and 11 short ES windows - full vs truncated at entry AND exit times, each with the sm2.HITS counters its code path must have bumped, and the switch -> executed-path check). The 42 variants also pass tests/test_bias_htf_lookahead.py.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#5/7)

1. `max_stop_pts` (item 44, built here for #83): which structural stop is "too wide" - a fixed number of ES points (default off) or a share of the account risk? The rest of item 44 (the second-break re-arm with `stop_mode="ce2"`) belongs to entries_stops_sizing.
2. `entry_style` (#69): the owner question "keep the pre-break OB (owner #11) as the default although ICT calls the raid an experienced-trader entry?" is for the owner; the tag lets the reports compare the two styles.
3. `lunch_org_ce_short` (#77) conflicts with T8 (no entries at lunch); research only. Confirm it stays an opt-in.
4. `dual_deal` (#78) conflicts with D15 / Ep 05; "split" halves gross / costs / net after the fact (the engine does not size the second leg). Which split is the intended test?
5. `unicorn_standalone` (#103) conflicts with M4 (a 09:30 sell-side run first); built independent of `ob_entry`.
6. `false_flag` (#64): "the latest significant swing" = the previous session's and the last five sessions' ranges. Confirm.
7. `mm_stages` / `mm_lowrisk` (#109 / #110): the stage-2 "OB MT" variant and the stop-out re-entry are not built; the stage entries use the breaker order and the BISI CE.
8. `ifvg_targets` (#84): the CISD is proxied by the MSS (`sw["broke"]`).
9. `pd50_bodies` (#86): drops the order (arm time) and cancels a pending one on a base-bar close under the 50%; with `pd50_tf` 5 only the arm-time test runs on the 5m bars.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, setups_models#5/7, chunk 5/7)

- #64 `flag_info`: the flag window is anchored on the FIRST sweep bar (`sweep_i`, as `near_miss_tag`), so a multi-bar sweep no longer pulls sweep bars into the flag; the pole origin is the highest high of the `6 x flag_min_bars` bars before the flag (the impulse, not an older high) [our interpretation]. Test `test_flag_multi_bar_sweep_and_bounded_pole`.
- #102 `stop_zone_gate`: (a) a pool beyond the sweep extreme in the stop zone that no bar traded (the real "unraided" case), (b) a level the sweep's FIRST bar did not take (multi-bar sweeps), probed by >= 2 wicks BEFORE the sweep with no body close beyond it; the sweep bar's own wick is no probe. `test_skip_when_pool_inside_stop` rewritten.
- #110 built as switches: `mm_lowrisk_reentry` ("" / "extreme" / "wick_ce": one re-entry at the FVG's near edge after the first order's stop was run), `mm_stage2` (stage-2 entry at the MT of an OB of the post-MSS move or the body of a reclaimed block; a leg whose entry price already traded is never chased) [our interpretation of the OB definition and of "do not chase"]. `mm_lowrisk` no longer needs a remembered same-side sweep (only the original-consolidation partial reads it); its orders carry sm5 kind "mm" (so `mm_flip` can flip back); an `mm_lowrisk` fill without a broken sweep arms no stage adds. Tests `test_mm_lowrisk_*`, `test_mm_stage2_*`.
- #103 `unicorn_standalone` now needs `ob_entry` "post_mss" / "both" as the spec says; the new switch `unicorn_independent` (default False) keeps the first build's independence (study variant `A14-unicorn-standalone_independent`); `cancel_close` at the breaker / FVG bottom. Owner question 5 is now this switch.
- #78 `dual_deal`: documented and labelled as a POST-HOC allocation (column `dual_alloc` = "posthoc"; the first fill's risk is not knowable at its entry). Owner question 4 stays.
- #112 `mm_flip`: the flipped leg trades MM-kind orders only (`x_mm_flip_kind` rejects any other setup); the positive flip unit test already existed (`test_mm_no_flip_by_default`).
- #108 `mm_accum_gate` (new, default off): the accumulation test (`sm5.accum_ok`) gates the original-consolidation and stage orders; `mm_accum_bars` alone still arms the standalone order.
- #99 / #111 / #107: `mit_stair`, `reclaim_ladder` and `mm_oc_entry` orders carry `cancel_close` at the block bottom / consolidation low; the reclaim-ladder kill is persistent (`_rcl_dead`).
- #98 `mit_cand`: an unknown midnight open rejects (it used to let the entry through unfiltered).
- #72 `second_dip_order`: the pivot filter is `q.known <= i` (was `e + pivot_n`, a look-ahead for pivot_n >= 3).
- #83 / item 44: `_finish_order` clears a stale `_wide_stop` flag at its start. The second-break re-arm and `stop_mode="ce2"` of item 44 stay unbuilt (outside this chunk; owner question 1).
- #69 / #70: `prev_pm_turtle` joins `setups.TS_KINDS` and the REVERSAL state family; `ts_linger_bars` no longer treats `turtle_soup="prev_pm"` as a sweep-driven turtle; `RAID_KINDS` lost the non-kind `false_flag` (a tag). The owner question about the pre_break default stays a question (no switch, by spec).
- #75 `org_reprice_gate` rejects (`x_org_reprice_org`) without an ORG today; the Params comment now says the ORDER is rejected. `stop_mode="ce2"` remains a study-variant setting.
- #86 `pd50_tf` > base: a pending order is cancelled by a completed `pd50_tf` bar closing under the 50% (`cancel_close_tf`, `sm5.tf_close_cancel`, counter `pd50_pending_cancel`).
- #84 `ifvg_targets`: still proxies the CISD by the MSS (owner question 8).
- Look-ahead coverage: new ES cases `hunt` (`ifvg_need_hunt`, window 2023-01-16..02-06), `mm_s2` (`mm_stage2`, `mm_lowrisk_reentry`; 2022-07-11..08-01), `uni_ind` (`unicorn_independent`; 2022-01-03..01-24), `gate` (`mm_accum_gate`; W3). A scan of every 21-day ES 5m window of 2022-2025 never reached `prev_reh_pools` or `ab_anchor_kept`: `prev_reh_plan` / `ab_anchor_lock` stay in EXCEPTIONS, now with direct full-vs-truncated checks at every bar (`test_prev_reh_levels_truncation`, `test_ab_anchor_truncation`, `test_ifvg_hunt_ok_truncation`); `dual_deal` is a post-hoc combine step, not an engine path. `news_two_sided`'s windows still prove the evaluation counter (owner-visible limitation).
- Docs: the commit message and the first build log said 53 new Params fields; the real count was 52 (the chunk's `NEW_SWITCHES`); this round adds 4 (`mm_lowrisk_reentry`, `mm_stage2`, `mm_accum_gate`, `unicorn_independent`) = 56. Footer note: the chunk-5 commit carries "Claude Sonnet 5.5"; later commits use the required footer.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#6/7)

1. Name collision: the spec's `consol_fade` (items 193 / 194) is already the delivery-state fade of chunk 2/7 (`consol_fade`, a different rule: a break out of a CONSOLIDATION state against the bias that closes back inside). The new range-edge / inner fade is `consol_edge_fade`; keep the two apart or merge them?
2. (Review round) `entry_discipline_only` (default True) makes the discipline presets exclusive as the spec text says (the FVG model after the MSS and every other path off); False keeps the first build. Confirm the default.
7. (Review round) `consol_edge_entry` defaults to "limit" (the rulebook has no market entries for reversal models); "market" is the first build.
3. `curve_short` (113) scans the short side against the bias when an untouched HTF array lies beyond the market; it is separate from `counter_scalp` (half risk, midnight-open exit). Combine them?
4. (Review round) `cc_ob_validate` now exists with default True (= the built behaviour); the spec's own default is False (no wait) - which default does the owner want?
5. Interpretations made for specs the sources leave open: the void-leg MSS entries (115), the premium-OTE band reading (119), `ts_cont`'s small-range test (121), the week / intermediate-term / NY / London-close definitions (195), the overlap ratio and marginal-gap thresholds (163). ### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, setups_models#7/7)

1. Name collisions: the specs' `profile_two_session` (item 217) and `profile_consol` (219) clash with the existing `profile_two_session` / `profile_consol_pm` of chunk 1/7; the new behaviour is `two_session_smt` (217, builds on the old switch) and `profile_consol` (219, which also turns on the old PM consolidation gate and swaps its test for the nesting test). Confirm.
2. Items 200 and 54 describe the same scalp with different triggers / parameters (`lc_adr_mult` 1.0 + `lc_retrace` 0.25 vs `lc_adr_k` 1.25 + 0.2): `lc_mode` ("failure_swing" = 200, "mss" = 54) keeps both; which one should be the default when `lc_scalp` is switched on?
3. D15 preference (never execute YM): `combine_exec_exclude` ("YM",) is always on; the "middle" pick (which ranks ES / NQ / YM) still ranks YM and only drops it if it is the middle instrument. Confirm that a YM middle means no trade.
4. `max_exec_markets` 2 raises ValueError for any hand-made combine of other symbols (the studies use ES / NQ, SPY / QQQ, SPX / NDX): 0 turns the cap off.
5. Interpretations made for specs the sources leave open: "not yet met" HTF array (201), the 5m zone / ADR5 target of the missed entry (202), the OR60 reference arrays and the "return" distance (213), the "first approach" of the AM array (214), the reading of "rebalanced" premium FVG and the side-space frame of the 09:50 branch (T7-0950), the sister-index delivery test (X14), the idea-intact conditions (X13b (b)), the offside thresholds (X13 reversal), the low-of-day level (X14), the session-state classifier (pairing), `cc09_depart_bars` and the room / scalp points (cc09), the NY-expansion percentages (nyx), the range-boundary test count (moc), the lunch-macro order cases A / B.
6. Spec defaults that would change an existing default: `ts_breaker_add_mult` (the spec says 0.5 x the initial units; the standard add fraction `adds[k]` differs for repeated breaker adds and custom `adds`, so the default is None = unchanged and 0.5 is a switch value - owner: which default?); `reentry_risk_mult` 0.5 is the old hard-coded half; `ts_stop` gained "mid", default "array".
7. Partial builds the owner may want finished by the owning packages: the Asia long clause (201, FX only), the full LUNCH session pick of item 13 (the setup itself is `lunch_macro_entry` through `special7`), the add-if-in-position of the 09:50 continuation (T7-0950; the engine holds one position at a time).

### Review round (setups_models chunk 7/7, caption-gap review fixes)

Fixed (majors):
- times#2 09:50 branch A (`sm7.rev_pool_run`): the premium FVG is judged in REAL prices - after a buy-side run a real bearish FVG whose CE lies above the real 09:30-now EQ (rebalanced = a body inside it, the next bar leaves it the reversal way), after a sell-side run a real bullish FVG below it (side space: a bullish gap with its CE at or under the side-space EQ, the exact mirror). The reversal order now runs toward the 09:30-09:50 extreme (`tags["fixed4"]` + `single_exit`, counter `m0950_rev_target`) = "the draw flips to the opposite pool".
- E5 missed entry (`sm7.missed_order`): the target is the nearer of the ADR5 extreme and the first 1h / 4h opposing array of the Confluence object (`cf.opposing`, side space; counter `missed_htf_target`); new `missed_kz` (default the futures NY kill zone 08:20-11:00) gates the order; new `missed_lon` / `missed_lon_ratio` build the London-lunch 05:00-07:00 variant (London quiet early 02:00-03:30, active late 03:30-05:00 [our interpretation]) as a `special7` job (the engine scans no session at 05:00-07:00); the 80 / 20 split keeps its quarter-ADR5 runner only when the ADR5 extreme is hit before 10:00 (`manage`: `runner_before`, otherwise the rest leaves with the first rung, reason `no_runner`).
- other#2 / #3 `backtest._combine_dual`: `exec_exclude` symbols are dropped before the dual keys are built (a YM row is no second market of a dual slot, never a dual row) and `exec_cap` runs on the dual symbols too.
- M4 breaker add: `ts_breaker_add_mult` default is now None = `_arm_add`'s own add fraction (byte-identical to before this pass for `ts_adds_breakers` / `ts_breaker_pyramid`); 0.5 is the spec value as a switch (the study variant sets it explicitly and no longer needs `adds=(0.5, 0.5)`).
- X13b `reentry_trigger="sibi_return"` alone: `sm7.loss_reentry_ok` routes the run_day gate through the idea-intact test whenever the gate OR the sibi trigger is on (the return order carries ext_i == stop_i and could never pass the restored-sweep test).
- cc09_003 / 022 / 046: `special7` passes the NY expansion, the 10:50 macro and the lunch macro through the `htf_bias` gate (`bias_now` in (0, side); counter `special7_bias_skip`); the London-close scalp stays counter-trend on purpose. The `session_veto` helper of other.md#10 does not exist (a different package): the equivalent checks (`blocked()`, `skip_news`) are applied in `special7`; sloppy / `block_side` are not.

Fixed (minors): win / trail-win re-entries reject market orders; `armed` is set only after the order tail returned an order (trail-win, reversal inversion / at_stop, sibi); `prop_fail` needs a body close through the propulsion MT (`prop_mt_lost`, from the trade's `prop_mt` lvx level; a trade without one counts as failed); the at_stop exit stays one body close and the exit reason `offside_rev` (documented: it is a reversal trigger); `adv_ladder` keeps each rung's own weight (5m CE 0.1, IFVG CE 0.3, IFVG bottom 0.6, renormalised over the rungs found) and the 12-bar window is now "formed after the sweep extreme"; `state_tag` stamps `Trade.state` on every trade (pairing "all" too); `lc_order` failure-swing stop is the DAY extreme + `lc_stop_pts`, the mss order has a forced exit at the AM exit (`exit_at`), `lc_retrace` / `lc_retrace_mss` stay two switches (the sources give 25 % and 20 %); `or_ref` and `am_array_open` invalidate on a body close through the array at any time since it formed (and a London FVG touched between 05:00 and 09:30 is not a first touch); `pos["trail_ref"]` is recorded only when the trail raised the stop; the PM nesting target is the near edge of the HTF array holding the AM entry (an AM trade still open at the PM cannot meet a PM order: one position at a time); `two_session_smt` adds the (720, 810) pair to a non-empty `smt_windows` itself and the hold test is `sm7.held`; the program's long of times#12 is armed in `lunch_long_win` (710, 730) only.

Deviations recorded: `nq_overshoot_mult` scales `sweep_ticks` / `mohawk_ticks` on one replaced Params per day (the spec's `_tick()` multiplication is not used; other `_tick()` uses keep the ES value, and a path that bypasses `run_day` does not apply it); `test_nq_overshoot_tick` / `test_cc09_day_trade_gate` live in `test_caption_gap_sm7a.py` (not `test_setups_models.py`).
Not built (recorded): models_setups_entries#201 Asia-long clause (FX only); times#2 add-if-in-position of branch B (one position at a time); the full T8 LUNCH session pick of times#13 (the setup is `lunch_macro_entry` through `special7`, the session machinery is the existing `lunch`); other#10 `session_veto`.
New switches: `missed_kz` ((500, 660),), `missed_lon` (False), `missed_lon_ratio` (0.5), `state_tag` (False), `lunch_long_win` ((710, 730)); `ts_breaker_add_mult` default None. Study variants: `E5-missed-entry-5m-retrace_lon`, `OT-one-state-tool-pairing_state_tag`, `X13-reverse-after-failure-switch_prop_fail`, `X13-reentry-after-loss-conditions_sibi_only`, `T7-0950-reversal-or-continuation_rev`.
Tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_sm7c.py` (19: missed target / kill zone / London lunch / runner, the dual combine with YM, the bias gate, sibi alone, win re-entry limit only, armed flags, prop_fail MT, breaker add sizes, state tag, lc exit, OR / AM array freshness, trail_ref guard, SMT window + hold, PM nest edge, lunch program window); `sm7a` updated (09:50 premium FVG in real prices incl. the mirror and the discount-FVG negative, adv rung weights, breaker add, defaults); `sm7b` gained the outcome cases (`lon`, `rev_inv`, `rev_prop`, `ts_re`, `time_d`, `time_e`, `time_f`; `time_a` / `time_b` moved to windows the bias gate still reaches) whose required counters are OUTCOMES (`re7_fill_reverse`, `re7_fill_ts_re`, `missed_5m_lon`, `m0950_rev_target`). Switches whose outcome no window reaches (ES 2022-01 .. 2024-06 in 3-week windows and 24 synthetic seeds): `reentry_after_win` (never armed) and `breakaway_reentry` (never two MSSs + a retrace): their evaluation counters run in the look-ahead cases, the outcome code is unit-tested (`OUTCOME_UNREACHED` in sm7b).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, entries_stops_sizing#1/3)

1. A12 OB level (`htf_ob_anchor`): the spec and the 2023-over-Core-Content rule make the OB `open` the default, but that moves the HTF-OB target levels of the DEFAULT engine; until the owner confirms, the default stays the built `extreme` and `open` is the switch (see the review round below).
2. A21 P/D leg: displacement leg stays default; implied range to target and post-CISD retrace range are `leg_pd_anchor` values.
3. D4 MT break: trade-based (028 / 036) vs close-based (B7 / 058 / 065A / 101) failure; default `mt_break="off"` keeps the built behaviour, spec default 'trade = warning, close = failure' is `"warn"`/`"close"`.
4. A12 CISD entry: M11b post-CISD EQ (`"eq"`, default) vs 032's open (`"open"`).
5. A16 PB invalidation: close under MT (default) vs any trade through it (`"wick"`).
6. A12 OB qualification: bare last-opposite candle (default) vs signatures (`ob_need_fvg`) vs full narrative (`ob_need_narrative`).
7. B5 prior NY session range is 08:30-16:00 [our interpretation].

### Tests (caption-gap pass, entries_stops_sizing#1/3)

`tests/test_caption_gap_es1a.py` + `tests/test_caption_gap_es1b.py` (69: defaults / validation, a unit test per spec, synthetic + gappy + ES look-ahead cases) passed; the generic `test_bias_htf_lookahead.py` for the 48 new DECISIONS variants passed. Existing files for the touched code: see the commit message.

8. (superseded by the review round below) `htf_ob_anchor` was briefly defaulted to "open" with a `study.PRE_OWNER` override; the review round reverted that - the default is "extreme" again and PRE_OWNER no longer lists it. Owner: switch the default to "open" (the 2023 Mentorship level; moves a default run's HTF-OB target levels, the golden window's t2 levels, 23 trades unchanged)?

### Review round (entries_stops_sizing chunk 1/3, caption-gap review fixes)

Fixed (majors):
- definitions#89 `htf_ob_anchor`: the default is the built "extreme" again (no hidden default change; `study.PRE_OWNER` no longer sets it; the golden regression reproduces without an override). "open" is the switch value; owner question 1 / 8 decides the default.
- definitions#42 `prop_fail_flip`: on a prop_mt / prop_wick exit the session now flips to the opposite side even when `only_side` was already set (it overrides), AND the failed propulsion block is kept as a reclaimed block of the flipped side: the order carries `es1.prop_zone` (real low / high), `es1.after_close` stores `state["pb_flip"][session]`, `run_day` exposes it as `ctx.pb_flip`, and `setups.array_cands` offers it as the "pb_flip" entry array (`es1.pb_flip_cand`; near edge = the first the retrace reaches, far edge = the stop side). Counters `prop_fail_array`, `pb_flip_cand`.
- definitions#7 `cisd_ob_px="open"`: a CISD bar that closes at / under the run's open no longer consumes the CISD - `sw["cisd_open_wait"]` keeps it and the buy limit at the open is armed on the first later bar that closes above the open (a body close under the run low voids it via the floor). Same for `cisd_adds` (`es1.cisd_add` keeps the run in `cisd_wait` until a close above its open). Counters `cisd_open_wait`, `cisd_add_wait`.
- definitions#131 `ote_nested`: engine tests with exact entries (parent sweet spot 99.18 vs the child's 99.885, a traded-under child replaced by the new lower child 98.9 -> 99.8145, `ote_parent_third` rejecting / accepting, no child = the parent run); the vacuous `>= 0` asserts are gone. A child can only be "dropped" in the engine by a lower pivot replacing it (a 3-candle low is confirmed on the arming bar), so "child stop-out -> parent" is covered by the unit test of `ote_children` plus the replacement run.

Fixed (minors):
- definitions#26 `add_pref_ob_in_vi`: evaluated whenever `add_mode == "fvg"` (also with no newer FVG); the sweep must precede the first fill (`pos["ext_i"] < fill_i`).
- definitions#4 / #87: the OB gates (`ob_need_fvg`, `ob_need_narrative`) now also guard the `array_cands` "ob" array, the "ob_post" array and the CISD OB (the other OB paths - reclaimed_ob, ob_secondary, HTF OB zones - are zone arrays of an HTF/previous structure, not a last-down-close candle, and stay ungated: NOT BUILT). A missing HTF array source is counted (`ob_htf_array_missing`).
- definitions#11 `mt_break` also governs the pre-break OB when `ob_stop != "mt"` (trade = stop at the MT, close / warn = far-edge stop + MT close / warning); `dict_mt` with `mt_mode="range"` is now the full range mid on the `_ob_at_low` path too.
- definitions#79 `leg_fib="body"`: the OTE band and the `leg_pd_strict` check in `ote_adjust` use the body leg too (`leg_pd_min` is not read by `ote_adjust`; unchanged).
- definitions#55: the implied range reads the draw when `single_exit` replaces t1. definitions#78 `ob_ce_confluence`: the 15m OB must complete at / after the sweep extreme [our interpretation: same move]. definitions#100/#102/#129/#130: the "ote" entry array obeys `ote_gate_4th`; scope notes in the Params comments (`ote_nested` is the FVG path only, `mtf_hold` acts only on bars finer than 5m).
- Look-ahead: ES windows that EXECUTE the outcome paths were added to `test_caption_gap_es1b` (`prop_flip`, `prop_flip2`, `prop_deep`, `out_overshoot`, `out_add_vi`: PB-wick exit + flip + array, deep-PB cut, VI-overshoot stop, OB-in-VI add); `OUTCOME_UNREACHED` is down to `ob_wick_ltf_entry` (0 of ~45 scanned 3-week ES windows reach a stubby HTF-OB wick holding an LTF FVG; unit-tested).

Not built / partial (recorded):
- definitions#92 `scan_tfs`: PARTIAL - FVGs of the extra bar sizes join the entry gaps; OBs on those timeframes, their projection as targets and the "meaningful point in delivery" filter are not built (only a 3m-only gap test was added: `test_scan_tfs_finds_a_gap_only_the_3m_chart_shows`).
- definitions#9 `ob_vi_overshoot`: the "short entries at the VI" part of the interpretation is not built (only the stop / exit is extended to the VI far edge; the VI must be a base-bar VI).
- definitions#39 `vi_tol`: only the helper (`vi_stop_level`) is tested at unit level; no engine test of a wick to the CE.
- definitions#4/#87 other OB paths, #42 array when the flipped side has no FVG context: see above.

Tests (review round): `tests/test_caption_gap_es1c.py` (12) + the new ES cases in `test_caption_gap_es1b.py`; `test_caption_gap_es1a/es1b/es1c` and the existing files covering the touched code were re-run (see the commit message). The full suite is NOT run here (once per package).

### Review round (second pass over chunk 2/3)

Fixed: (1) A12-mt-grading look-ahead (the grade used the fill bar's low / close): now fill-time information only + a post-fill regrade that scales later adds only. (2) E1-market-on-touch / chase: `convert_pending` drops an order whose stop was already pierced / gapped (the old code could fill under the stop and book a phantom gain; it fired in 2 of 9 ES windows). (3) E4 wick redelivery: fill price + closed bars only. (4) E4 colour recorded as a surrogate. (5) E5 chase: the standalone direct pick (`sm7.chase_pick` / `chase_order`). (6) E2 far-edge: the post-break stop raise + the 5m -> 1m edge note. (7) A13 higher-array / E12 zone ladder on the unicorn, cc and advanced breaker paths, intraday OBs in the array search. (8) B5: ValueError for a non-multiple m, scope documented. (9) A12 grade / dead-MT on the anchor OB. (10) close_proximity / fri_shallow: near edge forced, the gap must lie under the market. (11) One-shot add flags are spent only when the add was armed (post-partial, IFVG, early VI, VI-OB, IFVG-OB). (12) OB add-cutoff: the OB paths pass the body bottom. (13) Forced-path look-ahead books in `test_caption_gap_es2c.py` for vi_ob_add, vi_add_early_pts, add_day_mode (the real classifier, invariance to later bars), rb_ctx_mult (forced rb sweep key), e4_color / market_on_touch, the OB regrade. (14) `ES1M` mirrors the loader's data path (`backtest.DATA`).

Known gaps (not built): the FVG-path OB (`ob_blend`) has no grade; the array stops (`array_order`) have no dead-MT test; the session filter filters (no ranking) and skips HTF OB zones / prior-day OBs; `fri_shallow` has no breaker-upper-edge alternative; the far-edge raise needs a VI below the breaker (otherwise the first stop is already at the breaker bottom) and no ES window reached it (prefix-invariance + unit tests); the direct chase never occurred in 9 ES windows (the win state itself is `OUTCOME_UNREACHED` in sm7b; unit + prefix-invariance tests). Behaviour change in default-off switches only: `entry_order="market_on_touch"` drops orders that would have filled under their stop.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, entries_stops_sizing#2/3)

1. E12 add ladder (`add_ladder`): the spec makes 631 the candidate default once tested; the default stays "" (today's `adds` = 6/3/2/1). Which ladder is the default?
2. B5 execution timeframe (`entry_fvg_min_tf`): 15m minimum (cc12_004) vs the 2023 1m chain; kept 1 (the base bars).
3. E3 fill rule (`entry_offset_ticks`): the CC spread offset vs the 2023 1-tick-through rule; kept 0.
4. E5 / M3 (`breakaway_chase`): chasing vs 'better to miss'; kept off.
5. M10 IFVG stop (`ifvg_stop`): CE + 3-4 pts (built) vs beyond the whole IFVG (067); kept "ce".
6. E12 `add_mode` name: the spec's `add_mode` 'eq_cutoff'/'trend_day' collides with the built `add_mode` ("fvg"/"downclose"); the new switch is `add_day_mode`.
7. Interpretations: the A/B/X OB grade is judged with fill-time information and regraded after the fill (later adds only); `ob_arm="intrabar_htf"` is the base-bar trade-through; trend-day classifier; the high-volatility test; close-proximity / Friday draw proxy = the session lock.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, entries_stops_sizing#3/3)

1. E8 risk base (`alloc_frac`): the CC's percent of a 30 % allocation vs the 2023 whole account; kept 1.0.
2. E8 new-trader risk (`study.risk_grid`): 2 % ideal (cc02_001) vs too much (022); the default stays owner #12.
3. E6 stop depth: `three_pd` (098) vs `next_pd` (cc12_004); kept `ob_mt`.
4. E8-E9 5 % near an HTF level (research only) vs the 2 % maximum.
5. Interpretations: the dig, the "runs away" (0.5 x risk), the wick (>= 2 ticks, 5m, most recent), the void/CE band (+/- 25 %), "near" (an HTF level within 30 pts), the scalp class (single exit <= 1.5R), the step-up (+1 per 20 trades with a positive net) and "70 % consistent" (weeks), the deep run (n straight weekly closes + bottom / top quarter of 13 weeks), the frequency scaling, the probe (stop = session extreme, cut 50 %), old low "near", the two-way range, "tight" (3 pts).
6. `reentry_stop` already existed ("array" / "wick_ce"): "ob_low" is a third value, default "array" kept; `ob_stop` already existed ("mt" / "ext"): the spec's body / wick values are the new switch `ob_stop_kind`.
