### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, entries_stops_sizing#3/3)

- stops#25 E11-portfolio-exposure-cap | fixed | `max_exposure_pct` | 0.0 | now acts through Params: `backtest.run` applies `es3.exposure_filter` to the single book, `backtest.combine` re-applies it across instruments, and the three `study.py` combine callers pass `p.max_exposure_pct` (it also joins the "combine needed" condition). The decision is causal: the new trade AND the earlier open trades count at their `entry_risk_pct` (adds filled after the decision time are not known then; `peak_risk_pct` is not read). Test books have peak > entry; `test_exposure_cap_acts_through_params_in_a_single_book_and_the_study_variant`, look-ahead case `exposure`.
- stops#11 E12-repeat-dig-smaller-add | fixed | `add_dig_mult` | 1.0 | the multiplier uses the add's OWN FVG only (no guessed gap for OB / wick-CE / void adds; the retest add passes the entry zone via `dig_g`); when that FVG is the entry FVG only the digs after the fill count. DEVIATION (kept): the new-FVG add is armed on the gap's formation bar (no digs yet), so the multiplier bites the adds that name an older FVG (sm5 FVG add, `pos_g` re-adds); adds with an explicit size bypass it.
- stops#29 E8-E9-5pct-near-htf | fixed | `htf_near_any` (new) | False | the trigger is the DRAW being a 4h / D / W / M Confluence level (within 2 ES pts of the draw price) that lies within `htf_near_pts` of the entry [our interpretation: level kinds carry no timeframe]; `htf_near_any=True` = the broader reading (any such level in range; study variant `E8-E9-5pct-near-htf_any`). Adds stay proportional to the 5 % first unit (each add <= 5 %) with the near-HTF total cap: DEVIATION from "each add at 5 %" (owner question 4).
- stops#19 E12-mm-sell-model-adds | fixed | - | - | the retest closes must stay beyond the LIMIT level (the body top in side space, 104's "body bottom" of the short), not the looser body low.
- stops#12 E12-single-add-variants | fixed | - | - | "runs away" is measured from the OB body top (`es3.ob_top` tag of the pre-break order) + `add_mom_r` x R; only while no add exists. NOT BUILT: the 031 DXY / one-add variant (no intraday DXY data; the one-add reading is `adds=(x,)`, not an `add_max_n` switch).
- stops#14 E12-631-vi-wickce-invalidation | fixed | - | - | the pre-break OB order carries `es3.ob_mt` (the exit works on OB-before-break entries); a rung counts when its add FILLED (an expired / cancelled add leaves the ladder where it was, counter `vi_rung_unfilled`).
- stops#62 E6-propulsion-mt | fixed | - | - | the MT stop applies only to an order whose entry WAS moved to the propulsion block.
- stops#56 E6-new-trader-wide-stops | fixed | - | - | a tight-stop loss blocks only the same-direction re-entries (`reentry_half`, the turtle-soup re-entry `ts_re`); the reversal / win re-entries are no longer gated; the tightness reads the initial risk (`pos["es3_r0"]`).
- stops#58 E6-attempt-based-stop | fixed | - | - | a candidate order block the risk filters reject no longer ends the re-entry context; the next candidate is tried.
- stops#60 A12-ob-anchor | fixed | - | - | `last_open` also replaces the MT of the ob_run path (`ob_pick` other than blend): the MT is the midpoint of the anchored body. The FVG-path OB blend (`ob_blend`) is unchanged: SCOPE of the switch = the pre-break OB and the OB add.
- stops#36 E9-deep-discount-reduced | fixed | - | - | on a frame without the htf table the switch is inert: counter `deep_run_no_table` + one RuntimeWarning per process.
- Tests: short-side mirror test of the stop and add switches (`test_short_side_mirrors_the_long_result`); look-ahead synthetic case `exposure`.

Not built / scope notes (review):
- stops#23 E11-funded-account-caps: the daily-loss gate / flatten act in the bar loop; the Silver Bullet (`sb_model`) and Ma Deuce (`pre_open`) trades are produced after it and are neither gated nor counted in `state["day_net"]`. The sizing hooks (`class_risk`, `htf_near`, `deep_run`, `freq_ref`, `alloc_frac`) likewise act in `strategy._finish_order` only; `pre_open` builds its own add room (no `alloc_frac`). The switches are therefore partly effective in a book that includes SB / Ma Deuce trades.
- stops#30 E8-risk-by-trade-class: `class_risk` (scalp / day) acts in the engine; the swing (0.015) and position (0.01) keys have no consuming study engine yet (`es3.class_risk_pct` is unused outside its test).

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#1/4)

- A11 `bpr_gate_htf` (blocker): the gate now runs in RAW space in hook A (`raw_post`), where the rungs still equal the confluence / PDH values; hook B ran on exit prices (shifted by `exit_before_pts` / `pool_beyond_pts`) and never matched. The BPR is the nearest one with bottom > the leg high (`above`, the spec's rule). The unit test now builds its levels with `strategy._exit_px` and fails against the old placement.
- A22 `array_reuse`: `tp1.untouched` also scans the closes of the regular-session history since the array formed (a CE closed through on an earlier day spends it); test with a prior-day close. `"tiebreak"` stays as an alias of `"reuse"` (the ladder is price-ordered, an untested-first ordering has no effect [our interpretation]); the entry-side re-arm ("a second touch of a daily FVG arms") and the bias-flip invalidation are NOT built (the htf table owns the bias part).
- Look-ahead coverage: counters `speed_bump_tfs` (speed skip in force), `breaker_overtake` (body rule differs from the wick rule), `irl_outside_range`, `void_persist`; the ES cases `speed`, `breaker`, `range_irl`, `void` need them; the jobs-day drop (`nfp_no_htf_drop`) is reached by the forced test `test_forced_jobs_day_drops_the_htf_array_rung` (no ES window trades a jobs day with a daily rung above; the ES `nfp_no_htf` case proves the code was evaluated).
- A3 `merged_sweep`: only a lower, older NAMED pool merges a rel-eq sweep level (not another rel-eq). The `mark_levels` / lock-candidate consumer of the merge is NOT built (the lock reads the REH through `reh_merge` in `_targets`).
- A10 `vi_return_tag` is now the spec rule (far edge traded within 5 sessions, entry at the OPPOSITE edge). `vi_keep_days` sessions -> calendar days by 7/5 stays [our interpretation]. The void / OB fields stay in the Confluence VI rows (`vlo`, `vhi`, `obo`, `obm`): `htf._arrays` has no consumer of the ladder, so no second copy was added.
- A10 `void_ce_rung`: new knob `void_persist_days` (0 = the day frame only): intraday voids of the last N regular sessions stay open rungs until traded (variant `A10-void-levels-persistence_days`); the inert warning no longer claims `void_ce_rung` needs `vi_arrays`.
- N4 `eve_mode="bnb"`: the capped exit must pay >= `min_rr` x risk, else `x_eve_room`. "A+ setups only" NOT built (no grade-A filter exists for the eve; owner question below).
- L4 `lrlr_zone`: the L_b cap stands even when L_b is closer than `min_rr` (the ladder empties, no order, lv_tag `x_lrlr_room`).
- B19 `dxy_rally_cap`: the first rung is the first of kind array / pool. The gold hook stays a comment (no data).
- B14 `nwog_revert_targets`: the current NWOG is the last `nwog` (ETH) row of `ctx.gaps` (no `nwog_rth` / `ndog` duplicates); the "scalp" is the single-exit rung (tag `scalp_nwog`, the same single exit every scalp in this engine uses).
- A6 `measuring_qualify`: `levels.bk_state(c=closes)` - bodies filling the gap drop the label, a wick poke does not (the test now expects a wick poke to stay qualified).
- A22 `matrix_anchor="price"`: the premium list also holds untraded opposing FVGs (`fvg_targets`), order blocks (`ob_targets_1m` / `target_ltf_obs`) and old highs (`old_high_ladder`). Voids stay with `void_ce_rung`.
- L14 `erl_range_update="swing"`: with `irl_entries` an entry outside the rolling dealing range is rejected (`x_irl_outside_range`, variant `L14-dealing-range-redefine_irl`); the default stays "off".
- Still NOT built (each recorded above): 144(a) is an expectation without an order effect (no hook); 197 the HTF-range entry tag (item 168) and the grade-4 final rung = T are swing-work items the spec itself defers, grade 1 = O + 0.25 r stays an invented depth [our interpretation]; speed_bump_tfs Silver Bullet / Ma Deuce ladder (SB stays off); the 5 other partial notes above.

### Review fixes round 2 (caption-gap pass, entries_stops_sizing#3/3)

- stops#29 E8-E9-5pct-near-htf | fixed | `htf_near_add_literal` (new) | False | the spec's literal reading: the first unit and EACH add at `htf_near_risk_pct` (the add = that budget / its stop distance), the total capped at `htf_near_cap_pct` (study variant `E8-E9-5pct-near-htf_literal`; counter `htf_near_add_literal`). The proportional reading stays the default. - stops#23 E11-funded-account-caps | fixed | - | - | `es3.extras_loss_gate`: the Ma Deuce / Silver Bullet / NDOG rows are walked in entry order with the realized net of every row closed before their entry; an extra row entered after the net reached -limit is dropped (`daily_loss_extra_block`). Post hoc, extras only (the main loop's `day_net` is not fed by them; the main rows are not re-decided) [our interpretation].
- stops#12 E12-single-add-variants | documented | - | - | `adds=(x,)` is the one-add reading (no `add_max_n` switch needed). NOT BUILT: the 031 DXY gate (no intraday DXY data).

- stops#25 E11-portfolio-exposure-cap | documented | - | - | the filter is post hoc on the finished trade book: the engine's later trades (re-entries, loss-streak and day state) were generated as if a dropped trade had been taken, and open risk counts ENTRY risk only (adds ignored), so a pyramided book can exceed the cap. Docstring fixed.
- stops#36 E9-deep-discount-reduced | documented | - | - | futures-only (needs the htf table); inert with one warning on SPY-only frames.
- trader_mode "experienced" | fixed | - | - | `check_params` accepts it (committed with this package); test `test_check_params_trader_mode_values_cited_in_params_comments_validate`.
- Tests: `near_any`, `near_literal`, `reentry_ob` ES look-ahead cases added to es3b; unit tests for the extras gate, the class_risk warning, the literal add.
- Owner questions: (a) literal vs proportional 5 % adds (`htf_near_add_literal`); (b) narrowed near-HTF trigger; (c) exposure cap post hoc / entry-risk only; (d) daily-loss gate on extras is post hoc.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#1/4)

1. N-opposing-fvg-speed-bump vs owner #7: `speed_bump_tfs` (default `()`) makes the listed opposing-FVG timeframes neither a cap nor a bump rung when the bias agrees; the default stays owner #7 REVISED (15m and lower bump, 1h and above per `htf_resist_mode`). Which timeframes should count as a speed bump (5m only, 15m, 1h)?
2. N4-eve-of-high-impact: the eve size cut (`eve_risk_mult`, test 0.5) conflicts with E9's existing pre-CPI / pre-FOMC multipliers (`pre_cpi_risk`, `pre_fomc_risk`, `fomc_eve_risk`: they multiply as well); 042 gives no size cut. Keep one?
3. A22-array-reuse conflicts with the A22 / L14 "skip exhausted, prefer untested" (cc07_004): the default `array_reuse="first_touch"` keeps the built rule.
4. N4 eve scalp: should "A+ setups only" (042) filter the eve trade (the engine has no A-grade filter)?
5. B19 / BH-B19 DXY cap: conflicts with cc03_006 (Nov 2016 DXY and equities rallied together); default off.
6. `erl_range_update` default "off": the spec lists only the value "swing" (a possible default).

`tests/test_caption_gap_tp1a.py` (a unit or engine test per spec: defaults / validation, the study variants, the NFP-week split, the jobs-day filter, the eve scalp and day stop, the high-resistance count, the hrlr filter, REH merge, NWOG reversion, DXY cap, resistance class, post-raid gate and columns, EQ rung, runner slice, OR gate, speed-bump skip, measuring qualification, stubby wick, void / quarter rungs, array reuse, LRLR zone, leg MT, rolling range, breaker overtake, RB fallback, matrix price, polarity, OB location, swing grades, fulcrum week, VI void / OB columns, BPR gate, old-high speed rung) and `tests/test_caption_gap_tp1b.py` (the study variants, synthetic and gappy look-ahead books, ES-window look-ahead runs that must have executed the code path of each switch).

Engine glue in `mentorship/tp2.py` (hooks in `strategy.py`: the Params block, `_targets` (`tp2.targets_post`: session-pool filter, defended pools, the break gate, swept pools, event horizon, NWOG magnet, overnight 5m FVGs, HTF OB open), `_sell_side` (wrapper -> `tp2.sell_side_filter`), `_finish_order` (`tp2.raw_post` on the raw rungs, `tp2.skip_near` / `tp2.room` at the room check, `tp2.draw_prefs` / `tp2.session_first` at the draw pick, `tp2.late` for the ladder stage after the draw, the runner flag, `tp2_extras`), `mark_levels` (`tp2.lock_cands`, `lock_session_rank`), `_turtle` (`tp2.ts_weekly_bisi`), the position loop (`tp2.on_fill` / `tp2.manage` / `tp2.close_here`), `_exit_px` (`proj_exit_ticks`); `targets.py`: `sd_ranges` / `sd_levels` / `extra_sources` / `draw_pref` / `tf_wicks` / `fill_plan` / `_raw`; `levels.py`: `consolidation_band`, `long_wick_ces`; `confluence.py`: `level_score`, the OB `opn` field of `opposing_ext`; `setups.py`: `array_order` pick by score). ### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#2/4)

- #89 `proj_breaker_k`: the k > 1 rungs run from the swept swing low L1 (`sw["level"]`) to B, not from the sweep extreme L2 (k = 1 keeps the 070 formula from the extreme); test with L2 below L1. Still not built: a 60m breaker (the day-frame breaker is used) and the hold-to-the-week handling.
- #52 `sd_htf_extreme`: the closing SMT is evaluated on the bar that reaches the `proj_htf` rung (`tp2.extreme_smt`: the partner's highest high since the fill stayed at or under its highest before it) [our interpretation], not the entry's `tr.smt`; the last rung's `final_hit` is the session side done. Test: entry SMT False / extreme SMT True.
- #20 `nwog_ladder`: the rungs below the NWOG near edge stay (sharing the new `nwog_pre_frac`), only the NWOG tail is re-fractioned; `nwog_near_frac` 0 drops the near-edge rung (no partial); the far edge is scaled out in `nwog_far_split` (3) single-lot rungs. The gate "near edge first" is gone when the near-edge share is 0 (price inside the NWOG). Directive-9 tension (one exact target): `nwog_ladder` and `beyond_pool_final` rewrite the locked draw; both default off - see the owner questions.
- #101 `defended_filter`: touches are distinct visits (consecutive in-band bars count once; the pool's own pivot and its neighbours are one visit); tests incl. a lone swing high and a short side.
- #15 `eq_leg` "rally": no prior-day widening of the range (the leg itself is the dealing range); test with a wider prior day.
- #43 the minimum objective forces the long-wick test (`tf_wicks(force_long=True)`). Not built: the X12 `obj_stop` "idea counts as right" tag (also the #39 `obj_stop` source).
- #56 `proj_org_reprice`: the nearest `proj_htf` rung is the final exit (`final_proj_htf`), the variant sets `proj_exit_ticks=1`.
- #54 `sd_last_hour_k`: `proj_lh` needs min_rr x risk of room. The "short-reversal area in the last hour" (#53 / #54) is not built.
- #50 entry-low projection: the rung is inserted in sorted position among the rungs ahead (fractions split from the rung it displaces).
- SD-range projection kinds (`proj`, `proj_htf`, `proj_cluster`, `proj_runner`, `proj_lh`, `proj_wk`) are in `targets.SD_KINDS` (sd_close_hit, sd_align, fill_plan).
- #86: the slice at the first bar is capped at the last closed bar (no look-ahead). Still custom: the displacement test (not A20 `disp_mult`) and the evaluation at order build only.
- #53: the consolidation day is the DayPlan N8 tag only; with no premium level the pick falls back to the first level beyond the pool.
- #16 clear_path: no confluence table = not evaluable (no rejection). Lock-path limits (like #27 / #62): under the owner default `draw_lock="session"` the draw preferences of #16 / #17 (`session_pool_first`, `t1_skip_near`, the preference parts of `nwog_magnet`, `beyond_imb`) live in the non-lock branch and do not run; the linger tag is attached only with `hrlr_time_k` or `clear_path` on (it has no switch of its own).
- #41 not built: the cc04_012 "turtle soup to a daily FVG" ladder (T1 REH + 1 tick, near edge, far edge) and the daily-BISI sell-side delivery; owner question 1 stays.
- #60 not built: `only` / `extended` filtering of the `mark_levels` lock candidates (the substring classification of `lock_session_rank` stays). #98: inception stays the sweep extreme [our interpretation], not the extreme since the last opposite MSS. #89 / #39 `org_flow` counts arrays anywhere in the gap [our interpretation]. #45 (drop a projection when price retraces through the OB MT / BPR) not built.
- Commit trailer of the chunk-2 commit says "Claude Sonnet 5.5" (the session attribution); history is not rewritten, later commits use the required trailer.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#2/4)

1. L9-htf-fvg-target-ladder (010, 098, 102, cc04_012 far edge hit vs 029 blow-off only vs 049 / 061 / 065B "ringer"): `htf_gap_final` is "ce" in the spec; the CE-first gating is behind the extra switch `htf_gap_gate` (default off) because it would change the built ladder. Switch the gate on by default?
2. D23-inception-fulcrum-anchor: should the "fulcrum" anchor default (`sd_fulcrum_def`) become the inception-to-fulcrum leg (trade-through arming) instead of Ep06's close-beyond reading?
3. L5-nwog-target-ladder vs L9 (the far edge of a weekly gap is a "ringer"; also owner directive 9, one exact target - `nwog_ladder` and `beyond_pool_final` rewrite the locked draw): `nwog_ladder` aims runners at the far edge, `weekly_gap_ce_only` drops it. Which holds?
4. L12 first-level-only rule (014, 030) vs 043 (premium on consolidation days: `sd_premium_consol`), 047 (-3 SD in the last hour: `sd_last_hour_k`), 092 (deep convergence: `sd_deep_conv`): all default off.
5. L4-session-pool-before-rel (067) vs L4 "prefer the larger pool" (042, 049, 103): `session_pool_first`, default off.
6. L14-session-pools-only: "predominantly" - rank, only or extended?
7. L4-resistance-runs: the time factor drops a rung outright (`hrlr_time_k`); should it only tag and slow the ladder like `skip_high_resistance` "tag"?

### Tests (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#2/4)

`tests/test_caption_gap_tp2a.py` (a unit or engine test per spec on hand-made bars and stub confluence tables: defaults / validation, the study variants, the tp2.py helpers, `targets.sd_levels` for the SD switches), `tests/test_caption_gap_tp2b.py` (every variant builds; synthetic random-walk books and event-gap books, ES windows and forced hand-made days, each run full vs truncated at entry AND exit times with the executed-path counters; a defaults-unchanged run).

Not built / notes:

- Still not built (the review fixes above built the rest): #35 the BE+ move of "full_pull" (needs item 11, another package) and the high-resistance extra partials (X3f `resistance_mode`); #2 the fear / war-shock climate branch (documented only).
- Mutually exclusive ladder shapers (`ladder_preset`, `exit_mode2`, `partial_mode` new / terminus / quarters): setting two raises ValueError; `fixed_partials`, `no_collapse_htf`, `energy_split` and `pool_milestone` combine with them (milestone first).
- The new shapers use `p.ladder_fracs` as the base of any fraction they do not set (not the slow-day / news-ahead `_split` variants).

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#3/4, review of chunk 3/4)

- #35 speed_skip: the decision no longer uses the touching bar's own close. `tp3.speed_hold` marks the pool rung PENDING at the touch bar (nothing is paid on that bar), `tp3.speed_state` decides on the NEXT closed bar: the touch bar and the next bar both >= 1.5 x the median range of the 20 bars before the touch bar and both closing beyond the pool -> the share moves to the next rung ("skip"), else the pool is paid at its price ("pay"; counters speed_skip / speed_paid). A stop on the deciding bar ends the trade first (the unpaid share is stopped out - the conservative side). Look-ahead case `pay` (synthetic seed 6) requires eval_speed, speed_skip and speed_paid.
- #35 hold_runner exits at 15:00 (`exit_at` 900, the spec's "15:00-flat"; an earlier exit_at is kept); the `be_at` move already goes through `manage.be_move`, which never moves a day trade's stop (test). NOT built (dependency): "full_pull"'s break-even-plus move needs item 11 (`be_mode="through_opposing_fvg"`, management_exits package, not built yet); the extra partials of high-resistance runs need X3f `resistance_mode` (not built). The fear / war-shock climate branch of #2 stays documented only.
- #126 double tops: `tp3.dt_scan` scans the 1h / 4h buckets of the last 5 sessions (earlier sessions + the previous session + today, each bucket used once complete), groups the swing highs with `levels.equal_clusters` and pairs the neighbours of every cluster; `reached` (ever) retires the target, `reached_today` sets the x_dt_done gate; after the projection traded in the session `_lock_step` calls `tp3.dt_relock` and moves the locked draw ONCE to the nearest marked sell-side pool (counter dt_relock). The 4h buckets are the project's clock-aligned candles (00 / 04 / 08 / 12 / 16, as `confluence.py` and `strategy.htf_bars`), NOT the NY 01 / 05 / 09 / 13 candles: switching only this scan would make it disagree with the 4h arrays it is meant to sit next to [our interpretation] (the pair tuple carries its timeframe).
- #131 grades: the next grade is the final target only after `grade_through` (the grade below the entry was crossed by a close and held since the sweep) [our interpretation]; sub-range scoring tags sub_disc / sub_prem / top_seg; new switch `grades60_range_only` (off) = the range-bound gate (`range_bound60`, net move < `drr_net_frac` of the range).
- #132: the array that holds a quadrant / eighth 50 % line of the 60-day range outranks a nearer one (past_eq_quad_priority).
- #134: the "stop_run" orientation is DELIBERATELY the reverse of the spec's H - m x R: in side space the order is long, the sweep extreme is a low, and the spec's projection (below the sweep) can never be a rung for a long; the rungs are sweep + m x (pivot high - sweep) [our interpretation, commented in `ext_levels`]. New switch `proj_ext_sym` (off) adds the 100 % symmetrical swing. The default stays "leg" (owner question 6).
- #7: `adr_before_frac` default 0.15 (the spec's value; it only acts together with `adr_target`, off by default, so no default run moves; `exit_on` is gated on adr_target so the hook stays off). The check_params inert note no longer fires by default. Owner question 5 is withdrawn (no owner directive concerns the ADR exit).
- #159: T2 / extension rungs need `adr_target` (the warning was right, the code now agrees); #156: a projection inside the zone (+/- `adr_conv_pts`) that holds the ADR high converges; the finals have an explicit precedence adr_conv > past_eq60 > void > grade (first applied wins).
- #107: the unfilled test starts at the END of the leg (a fill inside the confirming bar counts); `void_legs` stays an independent switch (the spec says "with void_targets": the two target sets are separate, each works alone).
- #110: kept the price-ordered merge (the ladder walks ascending prices with a rung pointer; "inserted before other rungs" cannot be literal) - owner question 9.
- #119: "untaken" now looks at the earlier sessions of this week / month as well as today; the weekly pivot (a swing high of the completed weekly highs, untaken since) counts as a weekly pool.
- #169: the consolidation-day trade carries `exit_at` 660 (out at 11:00, not only no new orders). #170: the OR60 end is also a locked draw (`wide_or_relock`); inert note with htf_bias='off' in the Params comment and check_params. #172: `launch_arm` tag pm_launch_arm ("PM longs may arm there": no gate blocks such an entry elsewhere, so the permission is a tag only), the launch array is recorded once from 13:30 (`ctx._tp3_launch`). #175: hold_to_obj also exempts the trade from the PM time exit `pm_done_by` (`tp3_hold` flag) - the other flatten (session close 16:15) is later than the 15:45 `exit_at`.
- #2: the FOMC switch fires for 'beyond' and 'at'; the REH cluster rule applies to 'at' and 'before' and puts every sub-high at its OWN price (the highest keeps the mode's exit); `pool_exit` 'at' / 'before' replace an explicit `pool_beyond_pts` and the offset reaches every engine reading it (documented in the Params comment).
- #3 proj_kind overlaps the built `proj_exit_ticks` (L12-projection-exit-1tick, 013 ~29:08), which takes precedence in `_exit_px`; partly "already built" for the 1-tick case, `proj_kind` adds the SD kinds and the 0.25..1 pt buffers. Audit of the kind == "array" tests for the measured move (now kind proj under proj_kind): `ld2` efficient-gap drop / shield, `tp1` B19 first-rung, `manage.objective` htf_ce - none treats a measured move as a PD array, so no change.
- #37: `sweep_gate` uses the same 3 x ADR5 reach as the milestone rung; it needs a bias (htf_bias != 'off'); owner question 8 stays open. #38: the last rung beyond the HTF draw is capped at the draw (no_collapse_cap); the draw is read once at the order. #40: `energy_split` replaces the fractions of the other shapers (inert note).
- Tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_tp3c.py` (new: the fixes above, the SHORT side of void / measured move / OB order / weekly beyond / extensions / OR60 / double bottom via mirrored bars), the look-ahead cases of `test_caption_gap_tp3b.py` now require the executed-path counters (pay: eval_speed / speed_skip / speed_paid, dt: dt_proj / dt_relock, grades: grade_through / grade_final, past_eq: past_eq60_final / past_eq_quad_priority, daily_breaker: daily_breaker_cap, pm: pm_am_rb / pm_launch / pm_launch_arm, session_obj: hold_to_obj, wide_or lock: wide_or_draw / wide_or_relock under a fixed bias).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#3/4)

1. X3-new-vs-experienced-partials (065, 067): the spec recommends `partial_mode="new"` for the bot; the built ladder (50/25/25, 80/20 on slow days) is the default and D13's option B is ICT's experienced execution. Should "new" become the default? Likewise X3-full-pull-vs-mandatory-partial: `exit_mode2="pay_first"` is the spec's "candidate default" (default "" kept).
2. L15-min-3r-final-draw: CC's 3R floor (`min_rr_final`) vs owner directive "5 handles is the minimum room, the draw is the target" (owner correction #2) and the built `min_rr` 1: default 0 (off).
3. D13-speed-bump-ladder-004 (small 2/9 first partial) vs D13 option A (80-90 % at the first pool) and the owner #7 REVISED speed-bump partial: `ladder_preset="004"` stays a switch.
4. X2-pool-exit-at-or-before / X2-pdarray-exit-at-or-inside-edge: the defaults stay `pool_exit="beyond"` and `fvg_target_level="before_edge"` (003's unfilled at-edge limit is the evidence for "before").
5. (withdrawn in the review: `adr_before_frac` is the spec's 0.15 now; it only acts with `adr_target`.)
6. L13-cc-extension-projections: the spec's default anchor "stop_run" would replace the built `proj_ext` leg (MSS leg high) anchor: kept "leg".
7. L3-daily-target-weekly-beyond: "prefer / filter" - `weekly_beyond_mode` "tag" or "require"?
8. X3-pool-below-untraded-htf-partial vs X12 / owner directive 9 (one exact target): `pool_milestone` rewrites the locked draw's ladder (a pool milestone, the HTF edge as the final rung) - which holds?
9. X1-bullish-objective-order: the spec puts the three OB rungs BEFORE the other rungs; the ladder pointer walks ascending prices, so they are merged by price (a nearer pool is still paid first). Is that acceptable?
10. L13-cc-extension-projections: the stop-run extension is projected from the sweep through the pivot high (the side the trade goes), not beyond the sweep as in the spec text - confirm, and the default anchor (see 6).

### Tests (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#3/4)

`tests/test_caption_gap_tp3a.py` (a unit or engine test per spec on hand-made bars and stub confluence tables: defaults / validation, the study variants, the tp3.py helpers, scenario C through `run_day`, the FOMC-day exits), `tests/test_caption_gap_tp3b.py` (the study variants, synthetic random-walk books (partner index, event gaps, an FOMC window) and short ES windows with the no-look-ahead check at entry AND exit cuts and the executed-path counters, forced scenario-C days, the defaults-do-not-change-a-run test).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#4/4)

1. L15-min-3R-prefer-5R / L15-min-rr-3 (`min_rr_final`, now with `rr5` / `Trade.rr_final`) and new-rr-month-gate vs owner correction #2 ("5 handles is the minimum room, the draw is the target"): all default off; they stay study switches (chunk 3's owner question 2 is the same conflict).
2. X3-gap-speed-bump-before-pool (`gap_speed_bump_partial`, default 0) vs owner directive #7 REVISED (an opposing FVG before the draw is a speed bump with a partial BY DEFAULT): the directive's HTF-FVG bump is the built `htf_resist_cap` logic; this switch is the spec's separate small-gap (<= 2 pts) option and stays off - should it default on?
3. M14-tgif-management: `tgif_exits` True (25/25/50 on the first three zone levels, the built default of the TGIF model) vs "066" (L20 .. L30 with 15/15/50/10/10, the roll and the last-hour cover); the built one stays the default.
4. X3-fulcrum-exit-modes: the spec calls "ladder" the bot default; `fulcrum_exit` stays "" (off) like the other new switches. Which one is the default when the fulcrum anchor is used?
5. X3-mm-model-target: which edge of the original consolidation is "the near boundary"? Built: the edge the trade reaches first (side-space low = the real upper edge for an MM sell); the spec's test text (4110 - exit_before_pts for a 4100-4110 consolidation) reads as the real upper edge minus the buffer, which is on the wrong side of the level for a short.
6. L4-lrlr-ceiling: `lrlr_ceiling` (U = a swing_tiers old low a later close broke) is looser than the built `lrlr_zone` (needs the whole MSS pattern); both exist - keep both, or fold?
7. X3-layered-pool-ladder-runner: the collapse rule ("the first pool takes all units" beyond `collapse_adr_frac` x ADR5) is our interpretation of "depends on how big the move already is" - confirm the ADR measure and the 1.0 test value.
8. new-sellstop-run-before-void-fill: should a void within reach be a candidate of the session lock (`mark_levels`, owner #9) so that it becomes the locked draw, or stay a final-target pick below a free draw only?
9. new-rr-month-gate: `rr_big` = 5 and the relaxed floor 2 are ours; a stand-alone `run_day` / paper run has no month counter (always the strict gate).
10. A12-opposing-ob-time-of-day: the windows (10:00-11:00, 11:30-12:00, 15:00-16:00) are our reading of "profit-taking time"; only the A12 OB rows carry the 'ob' kind.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#4/4, round 2)

- new-sellstop-run-before-void-fill (147): `void_draw` now also applies under a LOCKED draw - a nearer unfilled void replaces the lock-side final target (`tp4.void_pick`, tag `void_draw_lock`; a locked draw nearer than the void stays). `void_rearm`: `strategy._level_intact` offers the swept pool of the re-armed session again once (counter `void_rearm_offer`; the gate in `tp4.late` still rejects any other pool / side). Status: done (owner question 8 answered in code; confirm).
- A12-opposing-ob-time-of-day (81): an OB rung that is the LAST rung is never skipped (its share would be lost; counter `ob_tod_last_kept`). Narrowing stated: only `entries.ob_target_levels` (the A12 `ob_targets_1m` rows) carries the 'ob' kind; the A22 matrix rows and the HTF OB rows (kind 'array') are not time-filtered.
- L4-lrlr-repeated-buys (122): the "no new long at the ceiling" gate now sits under `lrlr_zone` itself (`tp4.lrlr_zone_gate`: entry at or above L_b while the sweep extreme lies at or above the MSS level [our interpretation of "at the ceiling"]); `lrlr_ceiling`'s own gate is narrowed to entries within 2 risks above the highest broken old low. T1 stays the built `lrlr_zone` rungs (the untaken short-term highs inside the zone, capped at L_b); the retrace-origin swing high is one of them when untaken - not separately verified.
- T8-pm-carry-condition (19): `skip_balanced` counts the crossings from the bar that made the level known (a base-frame pivot's `known` bar), else from the day start [our interpretation]; `pm_carry_erase` uses `p.tick`; the AM leg origin stays "the lowest low before the AM high since 09:30" [our interpretation - there is no `ctx.am_leg` object].
- L15-min-3R-prefer-5R / L15-min-rr-3 / new-rr-month-gate: `rr_final` is recorded on every order that reaches `tp4.late` (scalp and fixed-target orders too, with the fixed single target as the final); the minimum-reward and month gates (`tp4.final_gates`) now judge the FINAL PLACED target after all cuts. The filter tag stays `x_min_rr_final` (not `x_rr_final`) so the built funnel counters keep their name.
- M14-tgif-management (75): the L25 rung is matched by index in the placed ladder (`tgif_l25_k`), the stop rolls to the latest swing formed AFTER the fill. "TGIF done is not set by a first L20 touch": no code sets a TGIF-specific done flag at an L20 touch - the session's done flag is set only by the order's close - so the line holds by construction; `tgif_reentry_pm` is the extra PM slot (counter `tgif_pm_slot`).
- X3-resting-1lot-partials-vi-final (45): fewer than three rungs = today's ladder (the invented 0.3 / 0.7 fallback is gone). X3-041 variant now sets `be_on="t1"`, `be_pad_pts=1.0`; the NWOG source is `tp2.nwog_zones` (the stored NWOG table), not `htf.gaps_for_day`.
- new-strength-no-return-discount-array (106): OB1 is the simple nearest down-close candle [our interpretation]; `hi0` uses only the closed bars before the fill bar.
- new-tf-matches-objective (140): T1 beyond the cap is rejected under a locked draw too (the ladder cut itself stays for free ladders).
- Not built / unchanged: M0-target-opposite-extreme-same-range - the 'asia' range (`ctx.sess` has no Asia range; the Asian evening is a separate window) is NOT built, and the nearer rungs are dropped (a price-ordered ladder cannot keep them behind T1); L13-multi-sd-breaker-confluence uses its own leg detection (`breaker_legs`: swing high, sweep extreme, closing break), not `levels.breakers` (the spec's A/B legs need the sweep extreme); X3-mm-model-target literal value (owner question 5, waiting); `capture_cut` / `runner_at_half` price the rungs from the planned entry (the ladder is built before any add fills), `half_range_ok` / `capture_b` use the realised average.
- Look-ahead coverage: cases h2 (`fulcrum_trail`) and j (`ladder_096`) now require their own counters. The paths consol_eq_target / consol_no_runner / tgif_roll / tgif_cover / void_rearm fire in no short real or synthetic window (scanned: synthetic Jan-May seeds 1-12 for void / tgif / consol, ES windows); they are covered by the unit tests in `test_caption_gap_tp4a.py` (and `tgif_pm_slot` fires on synthetic seeds 3, 4, 9, 10 on a 4-month window, too long for the truncated-run test).
- Process: commit 646c59b carries the trailer "Claude Sonnet 5.5" instead of the required "Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context)"; history is not rewritten (the cloud backtest is pinned); later commits use the required trailer.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, targets_partials#4/4)

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass) - management_exits#1/3

1. `no_entry_0825` (default True, as specified) only acts when a pre-09:30 window (`pre_0700`, `ny_kz_futures` PRE8, Ma Deuce) is on; with those off nothing moves. Keep it default on, or off like the other new switches?
2. BH-B14-NWOG-traverse-large-range vs owner #9 (one locked draw per session): under the default `draw_lock="session"` the "extend the final target to the next pool" part never applies (the lock target stays); only the T1-partial skip does. Should a traverse be allowed to release the lock?
3. N5-hold-through-0830: the spec makes the experienced version `hold_through_0830` (default False) while the owner rule says the EXPERIENCED version is the default. Decided: the flat at 08:29 stays the default because owner_directives.md line 16 ("No 08:30 entries; pre-09:30 setups are test-only switches") limits the effect to test runs with pre-09:30 models on; existing default (`pre_flat_0830` True: flat at 08:29) kept; the experienced hold is the switch. Which one is the default?
4. T9-strong-am-no-lunch-retrace vs owner #8 (everything out at 12:00): `no_lunch_retrace_hold` overrides the 12:00 close only with the switch (default off); the PM-entry extension is not built.
5. X2-best-case-resting-limit contradicts the X2 buffer (exit BEFORE the array) and the owner's "5 handles / the draw is the target" logic: kept as a study switch. The "momentum" rule (a range >= 1.5 x the median of 20 bars) is ours.
6. `heavy_gauge`: the T1 share is passed to the next rung when no opposing array was touched ("skip the T1 partial size cut (hold more)" read as: no T1 partial); alternative: a smaller T1 share. Which?
7. L4-resistance-is-delivery-speed: `resistance_def="speed"` with `resistance_mode` "low_full"/"both" pulls a fast run in full (collapses the rungs left into the last one) - ours; the plain tag is the spec. Also `resist_def` (structure / HTF, built in targets_partials #1) and `resistance_def` (this item) both exist - fold them?
8. L14-hold-above-reached-objective "sets the session side done": built as `done[session slot]=True` (no further trade in that slot that day) - confirm.
9. `be_mode` != "partial" switches `be_on` off for the whole run including the Silver Bullet / Ma Deuce when `extras_be_on="follow"` (they follow the main engine's `be_on`).
10. X5-day-trade-stop-no-trail: "raise_stop is blocked before 12:00 except via partials" - the built X5c day trade blocks every raise (BE, trails) all day; kept.

### Review notes (management_exits#1/3)

- Not built / narrowed: T9 PM-entry extension (209); the 1-tick-beyond-the-forming-candle stop (63); IOF new-high check and reversal tolerance (111); the position-trade engine (196, helper only); SB London has no 08:25 entry cut (its window ends 04:00); tick-match needs 1m (or 3m) bars - on 5m the switch is inert; breaker MT / wick-CE arrays are not in the heavy-gauge list (79); `fvg_full_fill_void` covers FVG / IFVG / implied-FVG entries only.
- Mid-trade tags: `Trade.mgmt_tags` (not an entry column). Order-time tags (`day_auto`) stay in `lv_tags`.
- Existing behaviour is unchanged with every new switch at its default: `exit_fill="touch"` returns the same price and threshold as before; `no_entry_0825` only acts with a pre-09:30 window on a release day; `flat_0830` equals the old `news(day)=="0830"` unless `news_claims` / `hold_through_0830`.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, management_exits#1/3, round 1)

- 11 N5-hold-through-0830: the PRE8 fill cut no longer depends on whether the position is held: `me1.pre8_cut(day, p)` = 08:30 on a release day under `pre_flat_0830` (also with `hold_through_0830`), so a PRE8 order never fills on or after the 08:30 bar. `hold_through_0830` stays default False (owner directive line 16, "pre-09:30 setups are test-only"); the Params comment says so; owner question 3.
- 94 A6-breakaway-by-refusal: `add_missed(pos, p, a)` counts only a pure time-out of the armed add (not t1_done, hold break, chop, `add_cut_passed`, `late_cutoff`) while price ran away: `best - avg >= max(avg - add px, tick)` [our interpretation of "without touching level 1 / level 2"].
- 209 T9: built the second bullet - `me1.lunch_ext` (strong 09:30 -> 12:00 rally, lunch retrace < `lunch_retr_max`, a PM bar breaks the lunch high) tags a new PM order `lunch_ext`; its `pm_done_by` 15:00 exit is lifted (the targets run to the 15:50 flat) [our interpretation of "no 15:00 cut"]. `am_exit_hold` never overrides an event flat (`ev_flat`: nfp_flat_by on a jobs day) and measures the AM leg from the 09:30 bars only (not the overnight low). Variant `T9-strong-am-no-lunch-retrace_pm_ext`.
- 63 X9: the "1 tick beyond the FVG-forming candle" stop is already built as `stop_mode="fvg_candle"` (middle candle extreme minus the pad) / "fvg_c1_low" (candle 1); study variant `X9-entry-fvg-immediate-rejection_forming_stop`. `void_pending` stays as a harmless guard (a resting limit usually fills before it can print through).
- 196: re-labelled "not built (spec only, deferred to M17)"; the study variant `new-position-trade-grading-fib` is a placeholder (pos_switch_frac 0.5 is the default, it changes nothing); no look-ahead coverage because there is no engine hook.
- 111 B6-intraday-iof: the default stays the Build block (a body close under the newest down-close open; any down-close candle is a support). New `iof_strict` (+ `iof_tol_bars` 3, `iof_min_body_ticks` 0) adds the Rule's conditions: each support must lead to a new short-term high within the tolerance and a low taken under the support candle must close back above it within the tolerance, else exit `iof_fail`. Variant `B6-intraday-iof_strict`.
- 79 B6 heavy: IFVGs (`sd.ifvgs()`) now count as arrays; the entry gap is excluded by its own edges (no `pos["fvg_top"]` needed); the `heavy_scale` partial is filled at the CE touch (min(open, CE)), not at the bar close. NOT built: breaker high / MT and upper-wick-CE arrays (no breaker object exists at the position stage; `_resist_live` is the HTF cap, a different thing).
- 62 A8 rip: a 'rip' now needs a candle that traded through the CE (a gap over the whole FVG is not a rip); new `rip_wick_mult` (> 0) adds large upper wicks (>= mult x the median range of the 20 bars before) as path objects like an opposing FVG (old FVGs of any age were already candidates). Variant `A8-rip-through-ce-signature_wick`.
- 11 X4-be-without-partials: `exit_mode2="full_pull"` now follows the opposing-FVG breakeven by itself (`me1.bemode`); every BE raise of `through_opposing_fvg` / fallback / `adr_frac` is capped one tick under the close; `adr_frac` moves to the average entry itself (no pad / cost). Variant `X4-be-without-partials_full_pull`.
- 5 X2: the bulk stays `best_case_bulk` of the units LEFT (65 % of n would swallow the remainder after a 50 % first partial) [our interpretation]; `best_case` is recomputed from the current last rung until the hold starts; the resting limit is also checked in `me1.manage` on every bar.
- 58 N10: `thu_block` now blocks from 09:30 for the rest of that Thursday (the extreme itself must be a 09:30-12:00 one). A blocked order cannot carry a Trade, so the tag `thu_week_ext` stays the HITS counter and the filter name `x_thu_week_ext`. The Thursday exit is at the close of the bar that ends at or after 10:00 (exactly 10:00 when the timeframe divides 60).
- 13 NWOG traverse: under the default `draw_lock="session"` (owner #9) the next-pool extension is dead by design (owner question 2); a traverse completed on the same bar T1 is hit finds T1 already paid.
- Stop locks: `trail_ok` now also gates the `bpr2_trail` / `wick_bias_line` moves (`_LD.manage`) and the tp4 runner / TGIF-roll raises; `eff_params` builds the effective Params once per Params object (cached on the instance) instead of `replace()` on every day.
- Bookkeeping: the SD time roll keeps the last SD rung as a normal limit exit before the window (the spec's "SD rungs are not exits before the window" does not apply to the final rung, which would leave the position without an exit) [our interpretation]; `hold_reached` marks the whole session slot done (owner question 8). The log heading is "Owner questions (caption-gap pass) - management_exits#1/3".
- Look-ahead coverage added (`tests/test_caption_gap_me1b.py`): ES cases `k`, `l`, `m` now require the evaluation counters `eval_day_pick`, `eval_thu_block`, `eval_flat_0830`, `eval_event_eve`, `eval_rip`, `rip`, `iof_exit`, `eval_be_cap`, `eval_runner_lock` (new `eval_*` counters: flat_0830, am_hold, day_pick, thu_block, thu_cap, vi_mid, rip, event_eve, add_expiry); a forced Thursday book runs `thu_cap` / `day_mode_auto` (real `day_pick`) in the full and every truncated run; the doji regression asserts exactly `["am_exit"]`. The rare situations themselves (sd_time_roll / sd_body_hold, vi_mid, thu_cap exit, lunch hold) do not occur in the 8-week ES windows; they run in the forced hand-made books and the unit tests.
- Found, NOT caused by this chunk: a look-ahead mismatch (a truncated run has one trade more than the full run at a cut) for `pre_0700 + ny_kz_futures + ma_deuce + sb_model + sb_london + pre_flat_0830` on ES 5m 2023-01-09 .. 2023-04-03 (also with `no_entry_0825=False`, and on the stashed HEAD, so it predates this review). Needs its own look.

Tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_me1c.py` (14 tests: the PRE8 cut, add_missed, lunch_ext / event flat / AM leg, full-pull BE, BE caps, heavy at the CE, strict IOF, rip, best-case resting limit, Thursday block, variants).

`tests/test_caption_gap_me1a.py` (defaults / validation, NWOG traverse, tick match, heavy gauge, lunch hold, N5 flat, Thursday cap), `tests/test_caption_gap_me1a2.py` (definitions specs: BPR limits, breaker MT, far half, CE exit, BPR backstop, VI midpoint, rip / stall, FVG void, resistance speed, IFVG 15m, refusal, dojis, IOF, position trail), `tests/test_caption_gap_me1a3.py` (ladder / stop locks: run-through, SD roll / body hold, hold reached, best case, exit fill, VI-then-SSL, opposing-FVG BE, HTF cap, ADR fraction, runner stop, day mode, event lock, trail confirm) and `tests/test_caption_gap_me1b.py` (study variants, synthetic and ES look-ahead runs with executed-path counters, an ES 1m window for the tick match, a Ma Deuce hold-through-08:30 run on real bars, forced scenario-C books). ## Caption-gap pass: management_exits#2/3

Source: `rulebook_gaps/management_partials_trailing_exits.md` items 20, 21, 23, 24, 25, 26, 27, 28, 29, 36, 49, 50, 52, 53, 54, 55, 67, 68, 87, 90, 91, 92, 93, 94, 95, 96, 98, 99, 100, 102, 103, 104, 107, 108. Code: `mentorship/me2.py` (the engine glue; same conventions as `me1.py`: side space, a bar is used only after it closes, every function behind a Params switch of the "management_exits #2/3" block). Hooks in `strategy.py`: `me2.on_fill` (after `me1.on_fill`), `me2.manage` (the position loop, after `me1.manage`; also `me2.chop_close` for the Chop detector), `me2.rung_taken` (the ladder loop), `me2.after_close` (`_after_close`), `me2.order_block` (the order stage, after the Thursday block), `me2.capv` (the stop cap of the three inline trails), `me1.trail_ok` (now also false while `pos["wgate"]` / `pos["freeze"]` / `me2["block_pool"]`), the `levels_frozen` guard of `_resist_live`; `newscal.macro_wins(t, p)` (15:45-15:50 with `pm_macro_runner`); `backtest.week_state` (`reversal_watch`, via `me2.week_watch`); `Params.dw_needs_week` (+ `stall_at_htf`). Shared pieces of the spec header: `me2.partial_close` (a fraction of the units left or an explicit lot count; lot rounding, at least 1 lot, all when fewer remain) and `me2.raise_to` (the stop tightener: never lowers, never on a day trade, clamped one tick under the close, refused above `pos["stop_cap"]`, honours the trail locks / wick gate / freeze for trails, logs `pos["stop_log"]`). The specs address the main engine's position loop: the Silver Bullet, Ma Deuce and the pre-open models keep their own loops and are not touched.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass) - management_exits#2/3

1. X5-tight-stop-under-swept-pool-near-target: RESOLVED in review round 1 - `pool_lock_pts` now defaults to the spec value 1.5 (0 = off); no owner directive conflicts with it. This is the one switch of the chunk that is ON by default (`me2.any_on` is true for the default Params; the unit books turn it off in `tests/test_caption_gap_me1a.py::P1`).
2. The new `ladder_trail` values (candle_mt / eq_pause / rej_block / 071 / structure) REPLACE the default FVG trail while they are set (one value at a time); the default "fvg" is unchanged. X5-no-trail-into-new-inefficiency (`trail_after_pool`) contradicts the default "trail under each newer FVG after each partial" - listed as a conflict, kept as a switch.
3. new-no-down-close-urgency: review round 1 follows the spec - the last rung keeps `runner_frac` (the rungs between T1 and the last shrink), no extra exit; the first build's T1 bulk exit is the option `urgency_bulk` (off). With only two rungs there is no rung to shrink, so nothing but the stop changes unless `urgency_bulk` is on. Confirm which is meant.
4. X9-lethargic-or-choppy-trade: `after_chop_exit` default "end_session" (022) vs "reentry_allowed" (cc03_003) - both stay; which default?
5. X8-profitable-stall-exit: `reversal_watch` blocks that direction for the rest of the week once the next weekday had no same-direction trade; "next session" = the next weekday; Wed / Thu reversal days are not modelled further.
6. X3-runner-stall-exits (b): the spec text says an equal high "against the trade" but its test is a long poking an REH; built as the test (side-space highs) by default, the literal reading (a poke under a relative equal low that closes back above it, "stall_rel") is the switch `stall_reh_against` (review round 1).

### Review round 1 (management_exits#2/3) - fixes

Findings fixed (code: `mentorship/me2.py`, `manage.py`, `strategy.py`; tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_me2e.py` + the updated me2b / me2c / me2d):

- #28 / #107 (central stop gate): `manage.raise_stop(pos, new, trail=False)` now refuses a level above `pos["stop_cap"]` for every caller and, with `trail=True`, also honours `me1.trail_ok` (trail locks, the overhead-wick gate, the OB-open freeze). Marked as trails: `manage.trail_extra` (first-FVG / breakaway / measuring), `targets.trail_breakaway`, `tp4` runner_htf and the tgif roll, `ld2` BPR raise, `es2` far-on-breaker raise, `es3` breakaway stop drop, `sm7` wick-CE trail. `me1._trail_confirm` is not a gated trail: its raise is the event that releases the trail_confirm block. BE-like locks (me1 be_modes / be_cap / runner_lock / event lock / best-case hold, setups, strategy add stops) stay exempt from the wick gate but obey the cap. The `strategy.py` inline "prior" / hit trails keep `me2.capv` + `trail_ok`.
- #103: `fulcrum_speed_bars` acts only on fulcrum-anchored trades (`sd_anchor == "fulcrum"`; with `fulcrum_exit` on, only the `fulcrum_ladder` shape - the "trail" mode has no pool). The fulcrum item (66) exists (`fulcrum_exit`, `tp4.fulcrum_ladder`); the first build log was wrong. Counter renamed `eval_fulcrum_speed` (it collided with tp4's `eval_fulcrum`); a forced day runs the path.
- #50: the peel is armed only when the NWOG is the order's target gap (a rung lies inside it) or a rung at its CE filled; `peel_last` is not wick-gated.
- #52: the next rung becomes the next untaken pool rung after the first partial (pool rungs already carry `pool_beyond_pts`); the +1 pt stop is retried each bar while the close sat under avg + 1 (the clamp kept it under the close). The runner is still made by trimming the levels at the fill (equivalent to "at the first partial").
- #90: the entry array is excluded from the three; the wick CE of recent large lower wicks is an array (`kind "wick"`, from `me1.wick_objs` of the other Side, mirrored); the proximity exception closes everything when price reaches A2 (`pd3_near`, every mode); the sister breach counts through `pos["sis_warn"]` (not `pos["warn"]`, which #94 also bumps). The spec numbers (A3 4097.5, `pd3_near_pts` 1, low 4098) are in `test_three_array_modes`.
- #94: the window is the touch bar plus the next bar (no confirmation two bars later; an undercut of the touch low on T+1 is a warning); opposing order blocks (confluence OB rows 15m+ and the other Side's blended OBs, MT as CE) join the gap rows. Test: `test_opp_tear_confirmation_is_not_accepted_two_bars_later`.
- #20: `pool_lock_pts` default 1.5; `no_reentry` and the day-done marker are set whether or not the raise moved the stop (`pos["pool_locked"]`).
- #108: no extra bulk exit by default (`urgency_bulk` off); the rungs between T1 and the last shrink so the last keeps `runner_frac`.
- #55: the stall is re-evaluated each bar (a new high ends it); `stall_reh_against` is the literal reading of (b).
- #95: the edge-bump add goes through `setups.add_ok` and the engine's add gates (adds_blocked, add cut, late cutoff, no add after T1); no broad `except`; E = the far edge of the opposing gap the entry leg traded over (not "formed in" it).
- #36: the draw prefers the array that contains the bias draw level (`draw_ord_lvl` of the htf row, via `bias_row_known`), else the nearest untouched array [our interpretation]; a planned rung printing at the array banks too.
- #29: the OB-MT-after-the-run rule needs an OB entry (`kind` "ob*" / "arr_ob*"); the candle is found by its MT under the running `mt_mode`.
- #87: an `ote="limit"` fill through `ote_adjust` on the FVG path arms the leave check (order tag `me2.ote_fill`).
- #24: only pivot lows at or after the fill count.
- #93: a failed reference is remembered (`rs_failed`) and not judged again; the lower-close counter resets consistently.
- #100: `reentry_allowed` does not set the half-risk flag after a chop-close LOSS when `reentry_half` is on (the engine's own block sets it); after a win it is set here.
- #68: documented - the day's low runs from the array start (the whole trading day), and a projection already exceeded before the fill tops up on the first managed bar.
- #98: the real-engine wiring is asserted by the look-ahead test (`eval_sister` counter); the broad `except` is gone (only a missing partner Side is a no-op).
- check_params: the dead `ladder_trail` / `mgmt` branch is removed; the new ladder_trail values act only with `mgmt` in ("ladder", "unit") like the others.
- Tests: short (side -1) mirrors of the hh_low, marginal, peel, stall_reh, three-array, opposing-array tear, fulcrum and running-down-equity books (`tests/test_caption_gap_me2e.py`); look-ahead: two more synthetic cases (three-array "close" / "into", chop_close, wick_ce_close_test, fulcrum_speed_bars, pm_macro_runner, trail_wick_gate "close", stall_reh_against), forced days for fulcrum_speed_bars and chop_close, ES cases b / g now take OB entries / the fulcrum ladder; the ES case f no longer needs the peel counter (the peel needs a target NWOG; `test_forced_gap_q_and_peel` runs it).
- New study variants: `X3-runner-stall-exits_against`, `new-no-down-close-urgency_bulk`.
- Commit trailer: commit 3c136df ends with "Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" (the harness attribution reminder); history is not rewritten. Commits from this round carry the trailer the task text requires.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass) - management_exits#3/3

1. D16-am-trade-held-into-pm (RESOLVED in review round 1: spec followed): `day_mode_no_am_exit` now defaults ON - day mode itself exempts the position and its pending order from the 12:00 close; `day_mode_no_am_exit=False` restores the old behaviour. Confirm the changed default for existing `trade_type="day"` / `day_mode_auto` runs.
2. X13-reentry-mechanics: ICT's cc02_005 enters at high[C] (limit at the validated candle's high), the built `reentry_stop="ob_low"` at its open. `reentry_mode="cc_ob"` is the high variant and keeps the stop under the whole low; which entry is the default of the re-entry?
3. N3-021-citation-misread (RESOLVED in review round 1: spec followed): the runner-stop refusal now applies whenever `reentry_after_win` / `reentry_after_trail_win` / `breakaway_chase` is on (`me3.runner_stop_on`); `runner_stop_no_reentry` alone turns it on without them and adds the final-rung session end.
4. X14-new-trader-stop-when-day-positive (RESOLVED in review round 1: spec followed): `trader_mode="new"` implies the day-positive / AM-or-PM stops (`new_day_stops` defaults ON; False restores the old meaning of "new"); `pm_recover_loss` was a dead switch and is removed (a losing AM is recoverable in the PM in experienced mode because nothing blocks it). Confirm the changed behaviour of existing "new" runs.
5. D18-reentry-count-max-2: the default stays one re-entry (`reentry_count_max=1`); the spec's switch name `reentry_max` collides with the existing turtle-soup re-entry cap (default 2), so the new switch is `reentry_count_max`.
6. new-laggard-slow-delivery: `backtest.combine(laggard_tag=True)` now adds the report column `laggard_draw` from the real draw map; the in-run flag that drives the longer windows is still the bar-based proxy (the partner's previous-day extreme) - the true "draw" of the other index (its own htf.table row) is only known after both instruments ran (`backtest.combine`); `laggard_mult` acts per instrument inside the engine.

### Review round 1 (management_exits#3/3 fixes)

- #116 day mode: exempt from the 12:00 close by default (`day_mode_no_am_exit` default True; False = old). #15: `trader_mode="new"` implies `new_day_stops` (default True); `pm_recover_loss` removed; `pm_after_am_win="draw_open"` never re-opens a PM that a stop / the day cap closed (`blocked_now` / `day_done`). Because of these two default changes `me3.BOOLS` no longer lists them; `any_on` tests `new_day_stops and trader_mode == "new"` (day mode needs no per-position state: `am_skip` creates it on demand).
- #9 `me3.runner_stop_on(p)`: the runner-stop refusal follows the win / trail-win re-entry switches. #13: `manage.any_stop_switch` includes `session_fail_flatten` so the flatten's `state["stops"]` entry is enforced without `session_stop_after_fails`; `_fails_in_family(skip=tr)` excludes the open trade. The "third PD array failure" stays the approximation (entry array failing + two prior failed trades; the X7c array-fail counter is not read directly).
- #128 `es3.ob_low_order`: the limit stays under the market (`entry < close`) for the high variant too; the default entry stays the built `ob_low` open entry (the high[C] entry is the switch `reentry_mode="cc_ob"`). #130 `order_block`: a setup without an MSS bar is dated by its sweep-extreme bar (`ext_i`); one with neither stays blocked. #124: the 04:55 and 06:55 partials are independent (bar-time convention: the bar's OPEN minute, the partial fires at that bar's close). #65: `_htf_level_below` catches only the missing-array errors. #1 `am_idea_fail` runs only inside the AM window (09:30 .. `am_exit`, 12:00 by default); the level is the nearer of the AM extreme and the pre-market extreme.
- #117 built the `two_session` branch: `setups.cc_profile(ctx, i, p, at=720)` classifies the AM leg from the bars closed by 12:00 (own cache attribute `_cc_prof_720`; the 13:30 call is unchanged). #111 built the combine-level tag `backtest.laggard_draw_tag` / `combine(laggard_tag=True)` (report only). #131 `paper.watch_open_trades` creates / ticks a `StopAckWatch` per open simulated trade and `paper.run` prints its actions (the paper layer places no real order; `acked` defaults to "acknowledged").
- Not built / by design: #199 "first trade still open" - the engine holds one position at a time and a second sweep of the low runs through the first trade's stop, so the stopped path is the only reachable case; #128 default entry (see above); #8 the 15:45-16:00 sputter cap is the existing `moc_cap_pts` (the `moc_run` target cap; no test of its own - `lh_done` ends the slot after an early MOC target); #13 array-fail counter (approximation).
- Look-ahead coverage: every exit / order path now has a required `sm2.HITS` counter in test_caption_gap_me3c.py (`eval_ir_top_raid`, `eval_vol_div`/`vol_div`, `eval_am_runner_hold`, `eval_explosive`, `eval_trend_hold`, `eval_session_fail`, `eval_pm_window`, `eval_am_idea_fail`, `eval_breakaway_arm`/`breakaway_fill_arm`, `eval_lh_target`, `reentry_ob_low`, `bpr_roll`, ...; cases `stops_bias`, `ote`, `lh` added); the synthetic `stops` case asserts its counters. The paths real/synthetic books never reach (offside flip, cc_ob count) run as forced scenario-C look-ahead tests in test_caption_gap_me3d.py (every truncation must reproduce the full run's trades). Not reached by any window: `lh_early` / `lh_late_runner` (no last-hour trade with a pool rung), `ote_ts` order, `pre_open_unfunded`, `recovery_lock`, `week_trail` - covered by the unit tests (me3a / me3b) only; their `eval_*` counters are required where the window evaluates them.
- Red tests on this branch (me1a2 `fvg_full_fill_void_through_the_engine` verified failing at 6c3ca48 as well; me1b `look_ahead_es[d]`, `forced_ladder_hooks`, sm7b `look_ahead_es[rev_prop]`, es3b `look_ahead_es[daily]` pre-date this chunk and sit in other chunks' code): independent of management_exits#3/3. The es3a trader_mode comment check passes.

### Review notes (management_exits#3/3)

- With every new switch at its default the engine is unchanged: `me3.any_on(Params())` is False (no `pos["me3"]`), `order_on` / `am_on` are False, `eff_p` returns the same Params object, `lost_side_holds` equals the old test, `count_reentry` stores `True`, `week_state` adds `flat_done` only after a `flat_week` exit. The only visible change is the new Trade column `laggard` (False).
- Names that clash with the specs: `reentry_max` (kept: the turtle-soup cap) -> `reentry_count_max`; the spec's `trader_mode` stays the existing sizing mode.
- `pos["ts_off"]` / `pos["stall_off"]` are set by `me3.manage` before `manage.exits` on the same bar and read by `me2` one bar later (its stall exits run before me3 in the loop): a one-bar lag, never a look-ahead.

## Caption-gap pass: news_calendar_times#1/1

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass) - news_calendar_times#1/1

1. N2-nfp-wed-after-11: `nfp_wed_cutoff` defaults to the spec's 660. With the default AM (09:30-11:00) and `nfp_week_block` off nothing changes; with a longer AM (`am_session="0830_1200"`) and `nfp_week_block` on, AM entries from 11:00 on the NFP-week Wednesday are now blocked (the new-trader rule). Confirm the default.
2. N1: the rulebook now says the first ~30 minutes after 08:30 (006 ~8:18; 065B ~4:12); `nfp_first_hour` (the name stays; default on: no pre-09:30 windows / Ma Deuce on jobs days) and `nfp_day` (default True, owner decision) are unchanged. Should `nfp_first_hour` be renamed / its 09:30 floor follow `nfp_day_from`?
3. N3-fomc-minutes: `fomc_minutes_mode="allow"` keeps today's behaviour (minutes days are normal days); cc10_008 ("block") and 082 ("pm_only") are switches. No default change.
4. T8-lunch-raid-target: three candidate pools are kept as `lunch_raid` values; which is the default once studied (025 "most recent", 064 "low before 11/noon", 075 "first swing after the news")?
5. N5-soft-bias-pre-open: `pre_open_bnb` (007, a Twitter-call example) now runs in side space (shorts mirror, test-covered), still on the base bars; owner directive "pre-09:30 setups are test-only switches" keeps it off.
6. T7 `macro_gate_set` defaults to `()` (every window of newscal.MACROS_T7, as before) rather than the spec's four windows; `MACRO_SET["1330"]` is three tuples (13:10-13:40, 13:30-13:35, 13:39-14:00), wider than the spec's 13:30-13:35. Both kept for continuity; confirm. `macro_gate_set` entries are now validated.
7. T8-lunch-raid-target: the trail guard is an `eq_tol` band around the pool, not "beyond the next swing"; `lunch_by_speed` moves the main engine's PM start only (Silver Bullet / Ma Deuce keep their windows). Confirm.
8. N9: the failure proxy is "no new extreme toward the draw AND no displacement candle (body >= 1.5 x the reference mean) in the window"; a failure sets the main and Silver Bullet done keys (all sessions with scope "day"); only a TRUE failure is cached. Ma Deuce is pre-08:00 and has no key to close.
9. N1/N2/N7: `nfp_first_hour` still the test switch (question 2); trend_day_nr (macro_trend_off) is its own "no retrace to the running 50% after 10:00" definition.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, news_calendar_times#1/1, round 2)

- X10 event_eve_noon: used `Confluence.targets` (beyond-price cut), which never returns the array price is inside; new `Confluence.arrays_any` (no cut), real-Confluence test, stub updated.
- N9 macro failure: displacement half added, done keys closed, False results no longer cached. BNB mirrored (no long-only skip). Vacuous `or True` asserts removed. `macro_gate_set` validated. Broad excepts narrowed. Study variants for the knob parameters (lunch_fast_pm / lunch_slow_pm, am_fast_k / am_slow_k, lunch_raid_by, fomc_end_min_pts, pre_fomc_week_mult, fomc_whip_frac).
- Look-ahead counters: all counters that the synthetic windows reach are now asserted. These paths need a real confluence / calendar day / market data that no synthetic or hand-made day builds, so they are covered only by unit tests and the ES windows (skipped without data): event_eve_noon, pre_news_coil, news_trend_side, two_driver_wait, fomc_whip_skip, trail_guard, pm_array_from, pm_1400, macro_trend_off, post_news_fade, pre_open_bnb, chop_wait, pre_draw_done_am, hard_day_done, kz_release_extreme.
- Not changed: `post_fomc_am="skip"` stays an alias in behaviour of skip_post_fomc_am (not remapped); `_mss_displaced` without a `broke` index passes; `am_session` already keeps the caller's `am` start.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, news_calendar_times#1/1)

`tests/test_caption_gap_nct1.py` (60 tests): defaults / validation / study variants, the calendar rules through `blocked()` (cpi_from, nfp_wed_cutoff, FOMC minutes, release counts, two drivers, post-FOMC skip, the already-built guards), helpers on hand-made books (news sequence, NFP both pools, FOMC stage A / B, FOMC end, trend day, lunch pools / wait / guard, AM speed, whipsaw, symmetry, SMT, coil, kz / sweep tags, macro failure, AM box, PM array time, pre-entry guard, chop wait, event-eve noon, fade / BNB orders, hard days, done flags, pool study), engine scenarios through `run_day`, and look-ahead runs (synthetic windows with Silver Bullet + Ma Deuce and executed-path counters `nct2.HITS`, hand-made days cut after every entry and exit, ES windows). Review finding fixed while writing them: `am_speed` and the coil's overnight range first read bars after the order's bar (caught by the truncated-run test); both are now read as of the bar.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#1/3 round 2)

- #55 thu_strict: the engine order dict now carries `broke` (the MSS bar), so the displacement check is live; test runs the real engine and checks the key.
- #56 fri_one_sided: the gate / scalp / tag need `i >= the 09:30 bar` (no read of the open or the overnight stats before it); the overnight high above Thursday's high is part of the condition; the tag `fri_one_sided` is emitted in `entry_tags` (modes "tag" and "raid_first"); the scalp is dynamic (`dwc1.scalp_now`) so the fri_t1_pts cap no longer depends on the order of the scalp reasons.
- #187 wk_profile: `mon_how` compares with `mh[0] == bias` (both directions tested).
- #37 august "light": single exit at T1 without the +5 cap (the +5 cap stays for the other scalp-flagged rules, per the spec's "scalp").
- #34 landmine: `_nfp_fomc_week(d, p)` honours `landmine_min`, so `monday_mode` and `monday_small_range` exempt a landmine Monday through the DayPlan.
- #43: rule (b) built as `sd_fvg_hold` (default True, only in `sd_intraday="rules"`): after the first close beyond the range, the first FVG in that direction and a retrace into it closing without a body through it. Counter `sd_intraday_fvg_wait`.
- #44: `P.info['sd_week']` follows the detector (flag and not released). Still NOT BUILT: the Thursday news raid and the post-array reversal setup.
- #211: `day_range` ignores the globex part (RTH ranges) when `n8_use_overnight` is False, so the trending / big-day classifications do not read the overnight session either.
- #35: with no partner instrument `post_holiday_am="symmetry"` keeps the AM blocked (counter `post_holiday_symmetry_nopartner`); the former owner question 6 is resolved this way.
- #11: the touched-CE test counts the Sunday evening low (`ctx.eve`); the below-wick scope is commented. #72: the flags cache is keyed on the CSV's mtime. #39: a test pins `calendar.SEASONAL_MONTHS` to the default `seasonal_map`.
- #159 parts (a)-(d) beyond the tag (FX leg, test trade, underlying trend outweighs) remain text only (NOT BUILT).
- Look-ahead tests added: a Friday raid-first day, a 330-session seasonal day (season_failed_gate / size paths), a `knobs` synthetic set with the previously uncovered switches, date-only `summer_off` / `skip_election` via `blocked()`.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#1/3)

1. N9-objective-before-lunch: the spec says `objective_done_skip` "must block only continuation, not the PM". The existing default (False) is untouched; `objective_done_pm="reversal_only"` is the corrected rule as a switch. Should it become the default of `objective_done_skip`?
2. B23 tags: windows overlap (short_may inside long_spring, buy_june inside short_may, short_jan inside long_fall). The primary window is the most specific one; the other tags are reported. Confirm the priority, and whether the regime tags (Jan-Apr / May-Oct / Q4) should overlap at Oct.
3. #159 renamed the weekly-tone tags to season_win_prime / season_win_minor (the quality tags of #38 already use season_prime).
4. #41: `skip_holiday_eve` stays a bool (old tests / presets); `holiday_eve_cases` ("a" / "ab") is the new switch. Same for #37: `august_from_day` was already 1.
5. #72 / #162: `macro_risk_dates` is a manual input; no flagged weeks are shipped (the study variants use a small example list MACRO_RISK_EXAMPLE). Supply the real list or a CSV.
6. #35: with no partner instrument the symmetry cannot be judged; the AM stays blocked (resolved in the review fix, confirm).
7. #56 / #37 / #43: the +5 handle scalp cap (`sloppy_scalp_pts`) still applies to the Friday (fri_t1_pts), macro-risk, push-range, day-4 and intraday S&D scalps; only august light exits at T1. `sd_fvg_hold` is a new switch default True inside `sd_intraday="rules"` (the spec lists the FVG hold as part of the rules).

### Tests (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#1/3)

`tests/test_caption_gap_dwc1.py` (48 tests): defaults / validation / study variants, the calendar rules through `blocked()`, helpers on hand-made daily books and one-day books (the real DayCtx + DayPlan) for every item, the report breakdown, one real-engine day with every switch on, and no-look-ahead runs (full vs truncated; synthetic windows with executed-path counters asserted, hand-made days, ES windows skipped without data).

1. #68: the spec asks for `no_long_after_thu_noon` and N10.4 `no_fri_after_strong_thu` "as one switch". Merge them?

4. #54: `trend_side_rule` replaces (does not extend) the first-pass side and "small" definitions when on; the table draw must agree at the bar. Confirm that a disagreeing draw means no trade that day (not just no side).
5. #55 / #77 / #71 / #73: parts of these sources are FX-only (DXY SMT, CBDR / Asian range, the London displacement wording, the 15m-body clause); they are replaced by the index-bot proxies noted above.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#3/3)

1. #90: the spec gives `tgif_window` the default "pm" and `tgif_min_adr` 3.0 with `tgif` on; both would change today's TGIF behaviour (no window, the 1.5 x average week rule), so the new defaults are "off" / 0.0 and the spec values are the switch settings (study variants). Keep that, or make "pm" / 3.0 the TGIF defaults?
2. #85: `tgif_week_high_exit` is True as specified, but only acts with `tgif` / `tgif_model` on (it tightens the TGIF order's stop to a tick beyond the week extreme and cancels a pending order there). Confirm it should be part of the TGIF model by default.
3. #25: `stock_intraday` False makes `run_day` return nothing for any symbol outside `dwc3.INDEX_SYMBOLS`. The set is the spec's eight plus the micro / mini futures, the bond symbols and the synthetic "RW" label; add / remove?
4. #10: the veto now writes `veto` rows into the skips list (reporting only; trades are unchanged). The bias `want` is still computed before the per-bar veto - it is not read before it, but a literal "bias after veto" reordering was not done.
5. #14: `lunch_kind_tag` defaults True as specified, so every PM trade's dw_tags now carries a `lunch_*` tag (reporting only).
6. #11 checklist / #20 / #31 / #11 narrative: several steps are [our interpretation] (the kz step for the PM, the "1h / 4h swing" stand-in for `mss_ref_tf`, "large" gap 5 pts, the trending-week size); confirm or give numbers.
7. #27: the carried "weekly draw" is the 4-week extreme (the same definition as `mw_carry`), not the htf.table's weekly draw; confirm.
8. #21 / #18 / #37 / #29: these skip whole days in `backtest.run` (and the gate stops arming in-day); the paper-trading loop (`paper.py`) does not carry the new week / month state (it passes only the weekly trade count).
