## Caption-gap review fixes: day_week_context#3/3

Round 1 review findings (items 93, 10, 27, 28, 90, 85, 175, 11, 7, 14, 20, 31, 33, 38, 25, 49, 21/18/37/29 and the look-ahead coverage):

- #93: the Ma-Deuce-only exemption (`md_only`) was unreachable (the "not re-delivered, premium half" state is exactly the state in which `bisi_two_sided` is False, so `inside_bisi` does not fire and the whole engine, Ma Deuce included, trades). The dead path in `_run_day_once` is deleted; the rulebook and this log now say so; `md_inside_exempt` only names the state (a test reaches it). `_inside_zone` became `_inside_zones` (EVERY qualifying zone; `bisi_two_sided` is True if any zone is two-sided, `md_inside_exempt` if any zone is in the exempt state), agreeing with `_inside_daily_fvg` when several zones qualify.
- #10: PARTIAL, recorded as such. Built: the calendar block + `skip_news` (`session_veto`) and now the per-bar sloppy stand-aside as a logged veto row (`dwc3.log_veto`, `(day, symbol, "veto", "AM:sloppy:chop")`). NOT built: folding `block_side` into the veto and moving the bias `want` after the first veto-free check - the side blocks are per-order (they need the order's side), and `want` only feeds reporting and the legacy day-level skip; the order of the other checks is unchanged (owner question 4 stands).
- #27: Wednesday to Friday are now neutral too once the carried level traded in the current week's completed sessions (`carry_neutral`, `want_override` -> 0); the carry side block and target pool stay Monday / Tuesday. The Monday session that itself trades the level does not go neutral (its bars are not complete). Still the 4-week extreme, not htf.table's weekly draw (owner question 7).
- #28: `study_week.midweek_turn_by_month(days)` / `dwc3.midweek_turn_by_month`: per month the weeks whose Monday + Tuesday range exceeded ADR5 (the five sessions before the week) and whose weekly extreme was set Wednesday / Thursday. The earlier "already built" claim was wrong (the weekly profile study has its own thu_reversal flag with a different definition).
- #90 / #33: the spec defaults are now the defaults: `tgif_window="pm"`, `tgif_min_adr=3.0`, new `tgif_need_htf_array=True` (same effect as `tgif_htf_array`: the `daycontext.tgif_context` array test; `tgif_precond="loose"` waives it; "tgif_htf_array" in `tgif_model_skip` also waives it). The owner directives do not mention TGIF, so there is no directive conflict. Every one of these acts ONLY with `tgif` (or `tgif_model`) on - the windows, the sequencing (`tgif_seq`), the branch (`tgif_branch`), `tgif_thu`, `tgif_no_rel` and the size gate - so `tgif` off is untouched. An active `tgif_window` replaces `tgif_timing` (`dwc3.tgif_window_governs`); under `tgif_model` the M14.3 `tgif_timing` windows stay authoritative unless "tgif_timing" is in `tgif_model_skip` (so the umbrella keeps its London-close + PM windows). `tgif_thu` now needs the large one-way Mon-Wed week (`oneway_week_so_far`: range >= 1.5 x the prior weeks' average, Wednesday closing in the outer quarter). "Target the nearer of the band or the opposite pool" was not re-verified: it is the existing `tgif_zone` ladder. Owner question 1 is superseded (spec defaults adopted; say so if you want the old off values back).
- #85: an open TGIF position is exited by the order's stop, which `tgif_week_high_exit` moves to a tick beyond the Mon-Thu / Friday-so-far week extreme (the stop IS the exit; there is no separate in-position rule). The reference includes Friday's own high up to the sweep bar, so it is mostly the sweep stop already - the switch is small by design.
- #175 (study): simplification kept and documented: the "discount array" is an alive daily FVG (not an htf.table monthly / weekly / daily array), the old low is the 4-week low, no monthly low, no premium-array overlap check of the 1.27 / 1.68 target, hit tolerance 15% of the swing [our interpretation]. Report only.
- #11: the ipda step now needs the session's first gated bar in 03:00-10:00 (a 10:00+ window fails; the PM follows `cl_pm_ok`); failing steps are logged as `(day, symbol, "checklist", "AM:dow")` skips rows; the result is on `ctx.cl`. The profile step still uses the D + 4h Confluence trend stand-in (`profile_tags` has no expected-profile object); steps 5-7 (fx) are not computed.
- #7: Silver Bullet / Ma Deuce / NDOG rows get the journal columns after they close (`dwc3.journal_extras`: a replay of the bars after the fill through the last exit bar, never a later one); a trade stopped on its fill bar reports `min_to_exit`, `mfe_pts`, `mae_pts` as None (not 0).
- #14: the lunch reversal takes the first MSS close AT OR AFTER 12:00 (`mss_after(..., from_t=720)`; an earlier 11:50-11:59 close no longer hides it); the duplicate `mss_after` is removed; the Appendix A Ep 05 line is corrected in place. Known limit: `lunch0` reads the AM lock's `side` / `target` at its first call (the 11:50 bar in the engine, where the gate runs every bar).
- #20: PDH / PDL = pdh1 / pdl1 only, NDOG / ORG gaps no longer count (NWOG kinds only); Confluence failures are counted (`htf_quality_conf_err`) instead of swallowed. Still price-matched (the order has no `level_kinds` yet); the `mss_ref_tf` stand-in is unchanged.
- #31: the 20/40/60-day extreme test in `htf_discount_hit` is a re-implementation, not `ipda_pools` (B21), and the B14 NWOG regime tag is not consulted; the Thursday / Friday tag stays `wk_hi_dow_n` / `wk_lo_dow_n` - stand-ins noted, behaviour unchanged.
- #38: `low_mag_season` is tagged on every date of `low_mag_months`. `low_mag_cap` still blocks through the DayPlan (functionally the same as the spec's `blocked()`).
- #25: `strategy.INDEX_SYMBOLS` is exported (the same object as `dwc3.INDEX_SYMBOLS`; the set is wider than the spec's eight - owner question 3). Vendor aliases (USA500IDXUSD, ES=F ...) are NOT mapped: they return no trades unless `stock_intraday=True`.
- #49: the strict template needs the intermediate expansion (Mon expansion, Tue reversal, Wed expansion, Thu consolidation, Fri reversal / retrace); the earlier rule is kept as `template_loose`. A Monday reversal cannot follow a Monday expansion, so the reversal day is Tuesday [our interpretation]. `study_week.week_state_labels` moved above the `__main__` guard.
- #21 / #18 / #37 / #29: `paper.py` now builds the backtest's rows from the earlier paper CSVs (`paper.paper_rows`), calls `dwc3.week_extra` (month_n, week_pct / week_pts, week_trades, the day-level skips) and `cap_day_trades`; owner question 8 is resolved.
- Look-ahead coverage: `test_every_switch_is_covered` now collects the covered names from the switches actually passed to the executed full-vs-truncated runs (LA_D3 / HAND_D3 / ES_D3), not from a typed list. The previously uncovered switches (`tgif_week_high_exit`, `tgif_min_adr`, `tgif_need_htf_array`, `inside_bisi_redelivered` with `bisi_ce_band` on the synthetic "bisi" window, `weekly_cap_pts`, `cl_days`, `cl_pm_ok`, `fav_pts`, `tgif_pm_from`, `choppy_week_days`, `low_mag_months`, `stock_intraday`) are now in those runs.

Tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_dwc3.py` (updated and extended: `test_carry_weekly_draw` Wednesday, `test_week_state_labels` strict / loose and the midweek-turn study, `test_tgif_band_target_am` defaults / windows only with tgif / timing override, `test_tgif_seq_loose` the Thursday precondition and `tgif_no_rel` with tgif off, `test_checklist_steps` the ipda window and the skips row, `test_journal_time_to_move` extras and None, `test_noon_reversal` from_t, `test_paper_rows_feed_the_budgets`, the look-ahead sets).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#1/7)

1. #12 (review round 1): the spec defaults are now the defaults (`nwog_flip_edge` "high", reclaim = daily close). The first-pass reading (edge "low" = 058, 09:30 price) is `nwog_flip_edge="low"`, `nwog_bias_basis="px"`. `nwog_bias` itself is off by default. Say if the first-pass reading should be kept as the default.
2. #8: the spec names a "daily bear FVG below" for the inversion; `inv3` uses the daily BULL FVG under the swing low (support turned resistance), `inv3_lit` follows the spec text literally. Which is right?
3. #45: `draw_order` keeps today's behaviour as the default ("nearest", owner directive #9); the spec's near-tie rule (fewer opposing arrays in the path, then the newer) is "nearest_tb" in the lock and the table. Confirm or make it the default.
4. #41: `skip_holiday_eve` stays a bool (old tests / presets); `holiday_eve_cases` ("a" / "ab") is the new switch. Same for #37: `august_from_day` was already 1.
5. #72 / #162: `macro_risk_dates` is a manual input; no flagged weeks are shipped (the study variants use a small example list MACRO_RISK_EXAMPLE). Supply the real list or a CSV.
6. #35: with no partner instrument the symmetry cannot be judged; the AM stays blocked (resolved in the review fix, confirm).
7. #56 / #37 / #43: the +5 handle scalp cap (`sloppy_scalp_pts`) still applies to the Friday (fri_t1_pts), macro-risk, push-range, day-4 and intraday S&D scalps; only august light exits at T1. `sd_fvg_hold` is a new switch default True inside `sd_intraday="rules"` (the spec lists the FVG hold as part of the rules).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#2/3)

1. #68: the spec asks for `no_long_after_thu_noon` and N10.4 `no_fri_after_strong_thu` "as one switch". The new switch is side-specific (the weekly-direction side only, Thursday from 12:00 and the Friday after a Thursday that closed with the bias); the old whole-Friday block is left untouched. Merge them?
2. #56: the spec's `friday_first_pool` names the existing "everything off at the first pool on Fridays" switch, so the pool choice is a separate switch `fri_pool_choice`. The earlier N10 wording "take most at the first pool" and this rule (lock the nearest unrun pool on the opposite side) sit together; confirm.
3. #48: "sick sister" in `range_trade` = an order whose swept level the partner index failed to take (`smt` True); the consolidation range = the last 3 sessions. Confirm both readings.
4. #54: `trend_side_rule` replaces (does not extend) the first-pass side and "small" definitions when on; the table draw must agree at the bar. Confirm that a disagreeing draw means no trade that day (not just no side).
5. #55 / #77 / #71 / #73: parts of these sources are FX-only (DXY SMT, CBDR / Asian range, the London displacement wording, the 15m-body clause); they are replaced by the index-bot proxies noted above.
6. #57: a Monday without a weekly bias is skipped under `monday_mode="big_into_array"`.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#2/3)

`tests/test_caption_gap_dwc2.py` (57 tests): defaults / validation / study variants for every switch, the item 125 already-built check, the plan / gate / side / order filters of every engine item on hand-made days (the real DayCtx and DayPlan), the position-loop helpers (Friday trail, ADR runner, ADR close exit), the reporting fields, the weekly profile classifier on hand-made weeks and on synthetic bars, the backtest day summary, and no-look-ahead runs (full vs truncated): five synthetic windows with the Silver Bullet and Ma Deuce on, three hand-made days, and seven groups on one 5-week ES window (skipped when the ES data file is absent).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#3/3)

1. #90: the spec gives `tgif_window` the default "pm" and `tgif_min_adr` 3.0 with `tgif` on; both would change today's TGIF behaviour (no window, the 1.5 x average week rule), so the new defaults are "off" / 0.0 and the spec values are the switch settings (study variants). Keep that, or make "pm" / 3.0 the TGIF defaults?
2. #85: `tgif_week_high_exit` is True as specified, but only acts with `tgif` / `tgif_model` on (it tightens the TGIF order's stop to a tick beyond the week extreme and cancels a pending order there). Confirm it should be part of the TGIF model by default.
3. #25: `stock_intraday` False makes `run_day` return nothing for any symbol outside `dwc3.INDEX_SYMBOLS`. The set is the spec's eight plus the micro / mini futures, the bond symbols and the synthetic "RW" label; add / remove?
4. #10: the veto now writes `veto` rows into the skips list (reporting only; trades are unchanged). The bias `want` is still computed before the per-bar veto - it is not read before it, but a literal "bias after veto" reordering was not done.
5. #14: `lunch_kind_tag` defaults True as specified, so every PM trade's dw_tags now carries a `lunch_*` tag (reporting only).
6. #11 checklist / #20 / #31 / #11 narrative: several steps are [our interpretation] (the kz step for the PM, the "1h / 4h swing" stand-in for `mss_ref_tf`, "large" gap 5 pts, the trending-week size); confirm or give numbers.
7. #27: the carried "weekly draw" is the 4-week extreme (the same definition as `mw_carry`), not the htf.table's weekly draw; confirm.
8. #21 / #18 / #37 / #29: these skip whole days in `backtest.run` (and the gate stops arming in-day); the paper-trading loop (`paper.py`) does not carry the new week / month state (it passes only the weekly trade count).

### Tests (caption-gap pass, day_week_context#3/3)

`tests/test_caption_gap_dwc3.py`: defaults / validation / study variants for every switch, the focused tests named in the specs (`test_consol_pm_fizzle`, `test_choppy_week`, `test_tgif_invalid_above_week_high`, `test_tgif_band_target_am`, `test_inside_bisi_two_sided_only`, `test_wed_news_reversal_study_smoke`, `test_weekly_po3_gate`, `test_journal_time_to_move`, `test_stats_acceptance`, `test_veto_precedes_bias`, `test_session_veto_refactor_identical`, `test_stock_intraday_guard`, `test_checklist_steps`, `test_pm_narrative_chop`, `test_month_cap`, `test_htf_quality_only`, `test_weekly_goal_stop`, `test_carry_weekly_draw`, `test_thu_after_targets_block`, `test_skip_friday_week_up`, `test_mw_extreme_array`, `test_missed_week_nearest`, `test_tgif_seq_loose`, `test_consol_pm_pool_exit`, `test_august_dd_stop`, `test_low_mag_season`, `test_macro_runs_study_counts`, `test_noon_reversal`, `test_pm_profile_study_labels`, `test_consol_overnight_tag`, `test_session_template_bins`, `test_week_state_labels`, `test_tgif_branch`, `test_consol_pm_news_skip_am`, `test_pm_mm_target`), synthetic full-vs-truncated look-ahead runs (4 sets), hand-made day runs, ten per-path causal cases (full / poisoned / cut) and two ES windows (skipped without the data).


## Caption-gap review fixes: day_week_context#3/3

Round 1 review findings (items 93, 10, 27, 28, 90, 85, 175, 11, 7, 14, 20, 31, 33, 38, 25, 49, 21/18/37/29 and the look-ahead coverage):

- #93: the Ma-Deuce-only exemption (`md_only`) was unreachable (the "not re-delivered, premium half" state is exactly the state in which `bisi_two_sided` is False, so `inside_bisi` does not fire and the whole engine, Ma Deuce included, trades). The dead path in `_run_day_once` is deleted; the rulebook and this log now say so; `md_inside_exempt` only names the state (a test reaches it). `_inside_zone` became `_inside_zones` (EVERY qualifying zone; `bisi_two_sided` is True if any zone is two-sided, `md_inside_exempt` if any zone is in the exempt state), agreeing with `_inside_daily_fvg` when several zones qualify.
- #10: PARTIAL, recorded as such. Built: the calendar block + `skip_news` (`session_veto`) and now the per-bar sloppy stand-aside as a logged veto row (`dwc3.log_veto`, `(day, symbol, "veto", "AM:sloppy:chop")`). NOT built: folding `block_side` into the veto and moving the bias `want` after the first veto-free check - the side blocks are per-order (they need the order's side), and `want` only feeds reporting and the legacy day-level skip; the order of the other checks is unchanged (owner question 4 stands).
- #27: Wednesday to Friday are now neutral too once the carried level traded in the current week's completed sessions (`carry_neutral`, `want_override` -> 0); the carry side block and target pool stay Monday / Tuesday. The Monday session that itself trades the level does not go neutral (its bars are not complete). Still the 4-week extreme, not htf.table's weekly draw (owner question 7).
- #28: `study_week.midweek_turn_by_month(days)` / `dwc3.midweek_turn_by_month`: per month the weeks whose Monday + Tuesday range exceeded ADR5 (the five sessions before the week) and whose weekly extreme was set Wednesday / Thursday. The earlier "already built" claim was wrong (the weekly profile study has its own thu_reversal flag with a different definition).
- #90 / #33: the spec defaults are now the defaults: `tgif_window="pm"`, `tgif_min_adr=3.0`, new `tgif_need_htf_array=True` (same effect as `tgif_htf_array`: the `daycontext.tgif_context` array test; `tgif_precond="loose"` waives it; "tgif_htf_array" in `tgif_model_skip` also waives it). The owner directives do not mention TGIF, so there is no directive conflict. Every one of these acts ONLY with `tgif` (or `tgif_model`) on - the windows, the sequencing (`tgif_seq`), the branch (`tgif_branch`), `tgif_thu`, `tgif_no_rel` and the size gate - so `tgif` off is untouched. An active `tgif_window` replaces `tgif_timing` (`dwc3.tgif_window_governs`); under `tgif_model` the M14.3 `tgif_timing` windows stay authoritative unless "tgif_timing" is in `tgif_model_skip` (so the umbrella keeps its London-close + PM windows). `tgif_thu` now needs the large one-way Mon-Wed week (`oneway_week_so_far`: range >= 1.5 x the prior weeks' average, Wednesday closing in the outer quarter). "Target the nearer of the band or the opposite pool" was not re-verified: it is the existing `tgif_zone` ladder. Owner question 1 is superseded (spec defaults adopted; say so if you want the old off values back).
- #85: an open TGIF position is exited by the order's stop, which `tgif_week_high_exit` moves to a tick beyond the Mon-Thu / Friday-so-far week extreme (the stop IS the exit; there is no separate in-position rule). The reference includes Friday's own high up to the sweep bar, so it is mostly the sweep stop already - the switch is small by design.
- #175 (study): simplification kept and documented: the "discount array" is an alive daily FVG (not an htf.table monthly / weekly / daily array), the old low is the 4-week low, no monthly low, no premium-array overlap check of the 1.27 / 1.68 target, hit tolerance 15% of the swing [our interpretation]. Report only.
- #11: the ipda step now needs the session's first gated bar in 03:00-10:00 (a 10:00+ window fails; the PM follows `cl_pm_ok`); failing steps are logged as `(day, symbol, "checklist", "AM:dow")` skips rows; the result is on `ctx.cl`. The profile step still uses the D + 4h Confluence trend stand-in (`profile_tags` has no expected-profile object); steps 5-7 (fx) are not computed.
- #7: Silver Bullet / Ma Deuce / NDOG rows get the journal columns after they close (`dwc3.journal_extras`: a replay of the bars after the fill through the last exit bar, never a later one); a trade stopped on its fill bar reports `min_to_exit`, `mfe_pts`, `mae_pts` as None (not 0).
- #14: the lunch reversal takes the first MSS close AT OR AFTER 12:00 (`mss_after(..., from_t=720)`; an earlier 11:50-11:59 close no longer hides it); the duplicate `mss_after` is removed; the Appendix A Ep 05 line is corrected in place. Known limit: `lunch0` reads the AM lock's `side` / `target` at its first call (the 11:50 bar in the engine, where the gate runs every bar).
- #20: PDH / PDL = pdh1 / pdl1 only, NDOG / ORG gaps no longer count (NWOG kinds only); Confluence failures are counted (`htf_quality_conf_err`) instead of swallowed. Still price-matched (the order has no `level_kinds` yet); the `mss_ref_tf` stand-in is unchanged.
- #31: the 20/40/60-day extreme test in `htf_discount_hit` is a re-implementation, not `ipda_pools` (B21), and the B14 NWOG regime tag is not consulted; the Thursday / Friday tag stays `wk_hi_dow_n` / `wk_lo_dow_n` - stand-ins noted, behaviour unchanged.
- #38: `low_mag_season` is tagged on every date of `low_mag_months`. `low_mag_cap` still blocks through the DayPlan (functionally the same as the spec's `blocked()`).
- #25: `strategy.INDEX_SYMBOLS` is exported (the same object as `dwc3.INDEX_SYMBOLS`; the set is wider than the spec's eight - owner question 3). Vendor aliases (USA500IDXUSD, ES=F ...) are NOT mapped: they return no trades unless `stock_intraday=True`.
- #49: the strict template needs the intermediate expansion (Mon expansion, Tue reversal, Wed expansion, Thu consolidation, Fri reversal / retrace); the earlier rule is kept as `template_loose`. A Monday reversal cannot follow a Monday expansion, so the reversal day is Tuesday [our interpretation]. `study_week.week_state_labels` moved above the `__main__` guard.
- #21 / #18 / #37 / #29: `paper.py` now builds the backtest's rows from the earlier paper CSVs (`paper.paper_rows`), calls `dwc3.week_extra` (month_n, week_pct / week_pts, week_trades, the day-level skips) and `cap_day_trades`; owner question 8 is resolved.
- Look-ahead coverage: `test_every_switch_is_covered` now collects the covered names from the switches actually passed to the executed full-vs-truncated runs (LA_D3 / HAND_D3 / ES_D3), not from a typed list. The previously uncovered switches (`tgif_week_high_exit`, `tgif_min_adr`, `tgif_need_htf_array`, `inside_bisi_redelivered` with `bisi_ce_band` on the synthetic "bisi" window, `weekly_cap_pts`, `cl_days`, `cl_pm_ok`, `fav_pts`, `tgif_pm_from`, `choppy_week_days`, `low_mag_months`, `stock_intraday`) are now in those runs.

Tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_dwc3.py` (updated and extended: `test_carry_weekly_draw` Wednesday, `test_week_state_labels` strict / loose and the midweek-turn study, `test_tgif_band_target_am` defaults / windows only with tgif / timing override, `test_tgif_seq_loose` the Thursday precondition and `tgif_no_rel` with tgif off, `test_checklist_steps` the ipda window and the skips row, `test_journal_time_to_move` extras and None, `test_noon_reversal` from_t, `test_paper_rows_feed_the_budgets`, the look-ahead sets).

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#1/7)

1. #12: the spec makes the NWOG flip edge default "high" (030 / 046 / 060); the built `nwog_bias_reclaim` default is "low" (058) and stays. Say if "high" should become the default.
2. #8: the spec names a "daily bear FVG below" for the inversion; the implementation uses the daily BULL FVG under the swing low (the support that turns into resistance). Confirm.
3. #45: `draw_order` keeps today's behaviour as the default ("nearest"); the spec's near-tie rule is "nearest_tb". Confirm or make it the default.
4. #41: `neutral_gate` "fifty_fifty" (reaction weights) already exists, so the REL / REH test is the separate switch `fifty_rel`. Confirm the naming.
5. #21: `org_tell` already exists as a string switch (the attempt-only tell), so the sequence tell is `org_tell_seq`; the 15m -> 1h -> daily target ordering is built as ladder rungs after the confirmed tell (see the entry).
6. #44: `allow_counter_trend` gives ONE counter-bias trade after the delivered draw; the spec's "per window, trade toward the nearest untaken draw" is not more general than that in the bot.
7. #1 / #6 / #15 / #59 / #65: the daily consolidation, lean, raid-gap and run definitions are [our interpretation] (comments in `bh1.py`); the minimum criteria are strict on real data.
8. #77: the joint ITH test is the spec's literal "close under the low of the last down-close week" (not the wk_dc trigger's own test); both must hold for the -1 flip.
9. Owner directive #7 REVISED (an opposing FVG before the draw is a speed bump) is not touched by this chunk.
10. Beginner vs experienced (review round 1): #24 `dxy_filter` (counter-DXY index trades allowed at reduced size = the experienced version; the spec default and the built default are "off") and #44 `allow_counter_trend` (counter-bias trades after the delivered draw = the experienced mode; default off). Both stay OFF by default per the spec; confirm that off is right or say that the experienced version should be the default.
11. #4: `wk_high_in` now follows the spec (any daily close below the guard MT); the first-pass fresh-break reading is `b7_fresh_break=True`.

### Review fixes (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#1/7, round 1)

- #10 BPR lean: the +1 mirror tested the wrong order; now bull FVG, overlapping bear FVG, close above the overlap (new clean test without a third FVG).
- #78 B9 persistence: the suppressed flip is pending and applies at the first later session where the swing low / FVG condition fails (test with the break sessions after the cross).
- #45 tie-break: counts the opposing HTF arrays between the price and each candidate (the old count was always 0), in `mark_levels` and `htf.table`; the tautological assert is replaced by tests through `mark_levels` (incl. the pool raided at 09:40 missing at the PM lock).
- #12 NWOG: spec parameters `nwog_flip_edge` ("high") and daily-close basis as defaults (owner question 1).
- #21 ORG tell: the 15m -> 1h -> daily ladder (`org_tell_ladder`).
- #8: `inv3_lit` (spec reading). #5: the nearest discount BISI only. #4: `b7_fresh_break`. #1: a new range starts after the breakout session. #44: pool-only exit, no reopen of a closed session or day. #16: a missing manual file warns. #77: `prepare()` warns without a flag map. #9/#18/#46/#47: couplings stated in the Params comments (`ceiling_ladder` warns without `vi_arrays`; setting both bellwether switches warns).
- Not changed (documented): the daily arrays of the lock hooks (`bh1e.daily_arrays`) are rebuilt from the hist regular-session candles (FVGs only, no OB / VI / weekly) rather than the table row's 24h-session arrays, so #5 / #10 / #15 / #16 / #41 / #59 level sets can differ slightly from the table's; the old `org_tell_seq` want override acts with `htf_bias` on only (the side gate always works); #41 stays the separate `fifty_rel`.
- Coverage note (#43 / #59 / #18): in the ES windows `iof_tf`, `min_criteria` and `bellwether_tf` leave no trade (the gates reject almost every counter-bias entry by design), so their look-ahead cases use surrogates (ZERO_OK) and the real paths are covered by the synthetic cases and the junk-future per-path cases of `test_caption_gap_bh1g.py`.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#2/7)

1. #95: `pd3_result` defaults to "flip" (the built behaviour, as the spec); the rulebook fix says three arrays closed through is an abandon (neutral). Say if "neutral" should become the default.
2. #116: `htf_bias_tf` defaults to "D" (today's reading; the 2023 "weekly first" is the weekly bias columns, "W" selects the weekly draw alone); "M" (monthly first, Core Content) is a switch.
3. #131 / #83 / #133: the concrete `qshift` trigger (new 60-session high, then an STL break) is a new column beside the older `qshift_tag` (60-session turn / consolidation); they can disagree. Confirm which one should feed the size rule (`qshift_size`).
4. #84: q_days counts from the latest daily MSS defined as a close through the latest known 3-bar pivot against the previous break; q_window = 40-60 sessions is the "last third" of the 60-session cast [our interpretation].
5. #109: the default is the spec's "the last 2 STLs each broken" (`tier_stl_mode="last2"`); "two_of_four" (the older build, less flicker with every new pivot low) is the switch - confirm which should be the default.
6. #143: oi_tag is keyed by the traded market (SPY / SPX / MES -> the ES file, QQQ / NDX / MNQ -> the NQ file); a symbol without a known market and without its own file gets no tag - confirm the mapping.
7. #88: the PM Silver Bullet after a winning AM trade is allowed only when the AM lock was re-locked by the breaker rule (`rev_after_target`); the main loop's own stop is unchanged.
8. #105: "reverse" is built for the bias reading and the PM lock; "add never in the expansion direction" needs no code (the engine does not add past the draw).
9. #131: read literally (a new 60-session high in the last 10 sessions, then a daily low under the latest known STL) `qshift` is active on 65 % of the ES sessions 2019-2026 (any pullback after a new high breaks the latest STL); the switch is off by default. Say if the broken low should be the swing low that started the leg instead.
10. Owner directive #7 REVISED (an opposing FVG before the draw is a speed bump) is not touched by this chunk.
11. (review round 2) #116: `htf_bias_pools` (the previous month's / week's high and low also count as draws in the monthly / weekly bias reading) is off by default (the literal spec: FVGs of that timeframe only); on ES the FVG-only reading was one-sided (91 % of sessions -1). Say which should be the default.
12. (review round 2) #136: the existing `first_clue` column fires after a raid of a 20-session high with a close back below, not after a raid of the HTF draw level (draw hit) as the spec words it; #135 `tug_spec` is the spec's tug (no new ground AND the tier regime), the older `tug` keeps its stricter post_raid meaning. Confirm or ask for the draw-hit tie.
13. (review round 2) #93: the speed is measured from the last confirmed weekly pivot (not from the week the draw formed). #82: the Monday / mid-week wait of the neutral week is not modelled (only `want == 0` or no weekly draw in the table). #88: the unblock of the PM family lives in manage.extras_blocked, which sb_model.run_sb calls (one place for all engines), not in run_sb itself.
14. (review round 2) #105: the persistent flip column takes the reverse side (the opposite state) after an HTF array draw hit; it lasts until the underlying flip state changes. #119: the season of the daily SMT follows item 60's window priority (15 May - 21 June is the bullish June-low window, so a bullish SMT there is "trend").
15. (review round 2) #131: qshift now expires after 63 sessions counting the trigger session (j - trigger >= 63); the literal trigger still fires on most ES sessions (question 9).

### Tests (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#2/7)

`tests/test_caption_gap_bh2.py` (defaults / validation / inert warnings / study variants / table configuration, the table-side functions on hand-made daily and weekly books, intermarket and OI),
`..._bh2b.py` (the engine hooks on the real DayCtx: bias readings, side gates, scalps / sizes / tags, lock adjustments and per-bar lock changes, the inside-BISI release through run_day, the B19 report),
`tests/test_bias_htf_lookahead.py` runs each new study variant (39) on ES windows (two needed a bias_neutral / surrogate pin: B17-hold, A23_15m), `..._bh2c.py` (the full htf.table on synthetic futures frames with every table switch and its no-look-ahead cut test, per-path causal cases, hand-made days cut after a bar, synthetic and ES no-look-ahead runs,
the coverage of every new switch). The ES windows skip without the data; nothing is platform specific.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass)

(bias_htf#3/7; review round 1 changes: items 3, 6 and the additions at the end)

1. #152: the spec's test text calls "TNX falling (notes up) with DXY rising" opposed, which contradicts its own formula (notes direction = -sign(yield slope), tandem when equal) and the rule text (notes and dollar moving the same way = tandem); the formula, the rule text and the second example agree and are built. Confirm.
2. #154 / #202: the specs extend `season_gate` / `allow_counter_trend` (bools built in earlier chunks) with string values; both are separate bools (`season_qualified`, `counter_range_extreme`) so no earlier default or type changes.
3. #147: `cot_gate="hedge_iof"` is now a PERMISSION (review round 1): an order that agrees with `cot_program` passes; an order against the program passes only when the hedge and the daily iof both agree with its side; with no program reading but a hedge state the strict hedge + iof test applies. The old strict reading (block everything without hedge + iof) is not kept as a value - say if you want it. #148: the loader needs the annual `deacot{year}.zip` files (owner download).
4. #184: the table draw already retires fully rebalanced FVG / VI / gaps; `retire_arrays` only tightens the weekly balance (daily wicks instead of completed weekly bars) and the two-sided VI. Say whether more is wanted (e.g. retiring OB or pool draws).
5. #174 / #222: "trend" on monthly bars adds a half-range net-move requirement so a flat year with a late rally reads as consolidation (the spec test); the weekly profile (#174) does not use it. #174 returns "" (no profile) when none of the three applies and below 26 weeks.
6. #200: the ADR5 target is the day's high (low) -/+ ADR5; the spec writes "day open high - ADR5" and also "open-of-day minus ADR5" for the reached test - say which anchor you want. "No array touched since 00:00" now counts only bars that open at or after the array was complete (review round 1). An existing farther same-side lock target is kept.
7. #190 conflicts with B16 (2023: no bias before Sunday): `week_pre` is off; both stay available as `htf_bias` values.
8. #221: `mo_aligned` needs ALL four components including a quarterly objective, so it is rare unless `q_dir_tag` supplies `q_obj_dir`; the held bias runs 63 sessions (84 max) from the last month-start reading.
9. #215: the mirror (bearish windows, a 60-session low) is our addition; #216 needs the partner frame (ES + NQ) - without it the poised map is absent and the window is left alone; the Dow (three-index SMT) is not loaded.
10. #225: `wk_struct_agree` is literal (needs `mo_bias`, so without `mo_bias_gate`-style data every order is half risk) - the switch builds `mo_bias` itself.
11. #45: "above the baseline gives longs" (continuation toward the next pool) is built; the spec words "the pool on the far side of the baseline from price" could also be read as a reversion toward the baseline - the reversion is `chop_target_baseline`.
12. The working tree held uncommitted bias_htf#2/7 review round 2 changes at the start of this step; they are committed with this chunk.
13. #154: `season_gate` is not extended with "qualified" (separate bool `season_qualified`); the notes / dollar qualifier reads `opposed` as qualifying a window of EITHER direction [our interpretation] - opposed holds on roughly half of all days, so inside a window the gate blocks counter-direction trades about half the time.
14. #202: with `counter_scalp` on, the `ctr` kind is ignored (the counter scalp is not limited to the range extreme); an inert warning says so. The str form of `allow_counter_trend` is not built.
15. #195: the reaction is a close at a session AFTER the touch session (review round 1); the "daily clearly respecting PD arrays" part is not modelled.
16. #225: the controlling ITL (ITH) is the latest one still unbroken as of the last completed week (round 1). #180: the reacting weekly bar must come after the OB's formation week (round 1); the mwd levels stay zero-height entry arrays in `_ts_arrays0` (not conf rows of kind dcandle).
17. #148: the hedge state needs 52 weekly reports (8..51: program only). #189: `pd60_only_consol` warns when `pd_day_range != 60` or bodies are off. #174/#222: the extra half-range `trend` requirement stays pending your confirmation (question 5).
18. Commit f8e921b also carried the bias_htf#2/7 review round 2 edits (bh2.py, bh2e.py, strategy.py, bias_day.py, tests), which were not reviewed with this chunk; history is frozen, so they need a separate review.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#3/7)

`tests/test_caption_gap_bh3.py` (defaults / validation / inert warning / study variants / table configuration, the CFTC loader and states, the wpr / profile / q_dir / held / month-week / in-play / iof-score / wk_pre / mo_bias /
wk_control / mwd functions, the Tuesday failure flip, the intermarket columns, the fall-low and poised maps, the checklist table columns, the loader feeding `cot_tag`), `..._bh3b.py` (the engine hooks on the real DayCtx and
DayPlan: COT gate, tags, stack, hallmarks, profile, in-play, bias_dh, premium wait, composers, sponsor gate, mo_bias / wk_struct, chop days, NY continues London, counter-trend, seasonal qualifiers, dcandle rows), `..._bh3c.py` (the full
htf.table on synthetic futures frames with every table switch and its no-look-ahead cut test, retire_arrays / pd60 / tue_fail in the table, the mwd glue, per-path causal cases, hand-made days cut right after a bar, synthetic
and ES no-look-ahead runs, and the check that every new switch is set in at least one executed run). The ES windows skip without the data; nothing is platform specific; no file outside the repo is written.

### Owner questions (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#4/7)

1. #113 / #184: the spec writes `htf_lifetime_wm=0` with "0 = no cap" as the default; that would let weekly and monthly arrays live forever by default (a change from the 60-session life), so the default is -1 (= as the daily arrays) and 0 is the spec's no-cap value; say whether weekly / monthly arrays should be uncapped by default. `htf_history_months` is also 0 (off).
2. #51: the spec's "otherwise allow longs only in the lower quadrant and shorts only in the upper quadrant" is built as: only OUTSIDE the 3/8-5/8 band (below it longs only, above it shorts only); inside the band with a bias and a locked draw both sides trade. Read literally the middle band would then trade neither side even with a bias. Also "bias == 0" is the `want_at` bias (htf_bias), "locked draw" the session's lock side.
3. #74: item (c) rejects any opposing D / 4h array between price and the draw, which is the opposite of owner directive 7 (an opposing FVG before the draw is a speed bump, partial there, by default); kept as a switch only. Item (d), the bullish seasonal period, is a plus and not coded.
4. #46: `pm_only` carries the AM draw through `carry_lock` only on a range-tagged day (it does not switch `pm_carry_draw` on); both switches together are the spec's "keeps the locked draw for the PM".
5. #168 / #170: the htf range is the weekly / monthly origin block's BODY top (bullish) to the nearest premium array or weekly swing high; the "newest origin block of either side decides" is our choice of the anchor array. `swing_ignore_eq` waives the `leg_pd` tests only (not `pd_zone`, not the `pd` bias).
6. #169: the spec's 0.8 / 0.3 asymmetry is built literally (longs at >= 0.8, shorts at <= 0.3), not mirrored (0.2).
7. #61: "with speed" is not measured; "holds" = two 15m closes above the CE since 09:30 [our interpretation].
8. #80: an IFVG bias lasts 20 sessions or until a close back through the other side [our interpretation]; the bias is only a fallback when the htf bias is neutral (it never overrides a bias).
9. #29 (changed in review round 1): the age of a swing pool is the number of sessions since its pivot bar; PDH / PDL / PWH / PWL are no longer tiered (they sit outside the tier test), so the W40 / W60 escalation can run with a previous-day level on the side. Say whether they should rather count as tier 1.

### Tests (caption-gap pass, bias_htf#4/7)

`tests/test_caption_gap_bh4.py` (defaults / validation / bx configuration / study variants, and the table-side functions on hand-made daily books and a 15m frame: range day, 60-session position, checklist columns, cadence,
daily Z, CE line, IFVG, trapped BPR, program, monthly range / flow, ladder, grid, poke tolerance, failed break, breaker regime, age tiers, event wicks, origin / mitigation arrays, OB to MT), `..._bh4b.py` (the engine hooks on the real
DayCtx and DayPlan), `..._bh4c.py` (the htf.table hooks on small synthetic futures frames: age tiers, weekly life, reach tolerance, event wick; the table with every table switch, the unchanged default table, cadence, the htf range
anchored at the origin body, monthly columns, no look-ahead of the rows, per-path causal cases, hand-made days cut after a bar, synthetic and ES no-look-ahead runs, and the check that every new switch is live in an executed run). The ES
windows skip without the data; nothing is platform specific; no file outside the repo is written.

Results: the 3 new files = 69 tests passed (ES windows included, the ES data is present locally; they skip cleanly without it). Existing files covering the touched code: `test_caption_gap_bh1c/bh2/bh2b/bh2c/bh3/bh3b/bh3c`
+ `test_levels_detectors` = 195 passed; `test_bias_htf`, `test_owner_directives`, `test_news_calendar`, `test_caption_gap_nct1/dwc1/es1a`, `test_day_week_context` = 327 passed. The full suite was not run (once per package, in the package Test step).


## Caption-gap review fixes: bias_htf#4/7 round 1

Fixed: #75 `FROZEN` now holds every weekly / monthly-derived table column (mwd, mwd_m / w / case / zones / levels, wk_dir, wk_po3(_draw), wk_q75(_confirm), iof_w, wo_*, wpr, obj_week, wk_* parts; a test scans the bias modules for new wk_ / mo_ / mwd columns); `weekly_midweek` refreshes only at the first row of Wednesday and treats NaN against NaN as unchanged. #29 the tier test covers the arrays and the swing pools only (PDH / PDL / PWH / PWL are added after it). #168 the htf range anchors only on W / M arrays no completed bar of their own timeframe closed through (`tf_arrays_alive`) and on weekly swing highs / lows not already taken. #170 the `swing_ignore_eq` waiver also covers the `leg_pd_anchor='target'` test (three tests in all, tested by a source count). #181 new switch `failed_consol_tfs` (("D",); add "W" / "M"): columns `failed_break_<tf>` / `fb_target_<tf>`, the merged `failed_break` takes M, then W, then D [our interpretation: weekly window = pd_day_range / 5 bars, monthly 6 bars, break within the last 4 bars]. #162 `matrix_tfs` W / M now feed `matrix_targets` (targets._sell_side and tp1.matrix_price through `bh4e.matrix_wm_levels`: the live W / M CEs beyond the EQ on the target side, as 'array' rungs); with `matrix_tfs` W / M and no `matrix_targets` the switch only tags and an inert warning says so. #113 / #172 the first-touch kill of a W/M OB (`htf_lifetime_wm >= 0`) is skipped while `htf_ob_to_mt` is on (the own-timeframe MT close test decides). #119 `bh4.bpr_list` calls `levels.balanced_ranges` (its pair scan now only walks the max-gap window with a bisect: same pairs, same order) and filters for large-range candles. #150 `_ladder_dirs` falls through a 0 to the next column per row and the ladder level joins the entry arrays in the no-confluence branch of `_ts_arrays0` too. #61 `cel_state` counts the 15m closes from 09:30 as documented. #173 the reach tolerance widens `_htf_tag` on the approach side (the zone top in side space) only. #184 `paper.history_days(minutes, p)` and `paper.short_history` follow `htf_history_months`; `backtest.hist_days` also covers `pd_grid_days` (the body range). Look-ahead coverage: `htf_history_months=18`, `failed_consol_tfs=(D, W, M)` and `matrix_targets` joined the table / engine look-ahead sets.

Still not built (honest list): #80 the HTF "gap traded fully both ways" candidate level; #139 a list of known event dates (event wicks are detected by size only); #145 the lethargic-response cut-size / exit step (X9); #150 the cc05_016 buy stop and the 4h ladder levels (daily only); #162 the W / M columns are in the target list only behind `matrix_targets`; #168 the distance-based management thresholds are not rewired to the htf range; #177 the first-profit target at a 4h / 1h premium array and the allowance of a quadrant-discount long inside the overall premium (tags only); #178 the tag is on the order's entry, not the 4h OB zone; #182 the "confirm with respected bullish OBs" step; #184 transposing all arrays to the 4h chart; #185 the breaker retest as the primary entry with no deeper OTE; #61 the sell-side / NWOG / prior-close targets of the CE-line read; #110 the table columns eq_d / eq_4h / eq_1h / eq_15m (computed per order); #74 seasonal item (d); #170 the `pd_zone` / `pd` bias reading is a table-side reading, not an order gate, so nothing is waived there.

Owner questions added: (10) #113 / #172: with `htf_lifetime_wm >= 0` the first-touch kill and `htf_ob_to_mt` conflict; `htf_ob_to_mt` wins (the kill is skipped). (11) #113: the code default of `htf_lifetime_wm` stays -1 (the old behaviour); the spec's default is 0 (no cap); say which you want. (12) #51: the quadrant rule is built at the 3/8 and 5/8 band edges, not at 1/4 and 3/4; a long at 0.25-0.375 is allowed; say which boundary you want. (13) #46: `pm_only` carries the AM draw only on a range-tagged day (already question 4). (14) Commit trailer of 8711d94 reads "Claude Sonnet 5.5" instead of the required "Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context)"; fixing it needs a history rewrite, which is forbidden here.

Tests: `tests/test_caption_gap_bh4d.py` (12 tests: PDH outside the tiers, the live-only htf range, the weekly / monthly failed break, matrix W / M targets, the first-touch kill vs the MT test, the ladder fall-through, the BPR through balanced_ranges, the one-sided reach tolerance, the paper history, the waiver wiring) plus the new cadence tests in `tests/test_caption_gap_bh4.py`; the look-ahead sets of `test_caption_gap_bh4c.py` were widened. Ran: the four bh4 files (81 passed) and the covering files `test_levels_detectors`, `test_levels_review`, `test_caption_gap_bh1c/bh2/bh3/bh3b`, `test_bias_htf`, `test_owner_directives`. The full suite was not run.
